When Flower Petals Fall 2: Player's Bible

Exported 2026-10-26 · 236 notes

Sessions

Session 1

Canon Party meets in Benz during Starsfall; a corrupted deity's manifestation falls as a star and releases Star-Fallen creatures; defeating them activates God of Second Chances fragments in each party member; the Thorns massacre witnesses and frame the party; the fugitives flee after sharing a divine vision

Session 1

Quick Reference

Session #: 1

Played On:

In-world Date: 7 Pollant 301 Bloom

PCs Present: Calanthe Orchidaceae, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Layla, Cleke

Recap

A read-aloud paragraph: what happened and what it meant. The party arrives separately in Benz during the Festival of Shooting Stars. A fallen star crashes into the village, releasing alien creatures that kill many inhabitants. The party unites and defeats the creatures. The The Thorns arrive shortly after to take statements from survivors, then secretly kill every witness. The party notices and escapes before they can be silenced. The Crown charges the party with Treason, Sedition, Arson, and other crimes and publicly attributes the destruction of Benz to a terrorist attack by the party. The falling star was not a natural phenomenon. It was a corrupted divine manifestation of an unnamed god, driven to Benz while being hunted by the Thorns. When the party destroyed the Star-Fallen creatures it released, the divine energy flooding the area activated inert fragments of the God of Second Chances each member happened to carry by coincidence. The Thorns arrived and killed every surviving witness. Every party member then shared the same vision: "Find the others... before she does." They fled Benz as fugitives, formally charged with sedition, arson, and treason.

  • The events unfold during Starsfall (7 Pollant). The Crown later used the incident to publicly frame the party: see Party Blamed for the Benz Incident.

Key Events

The beats worth remembering, as bullets.

  • 7 Pollant 301 Bloom: Cleke, arriving at the end of his outward journey from the Appori Enclave, meets the rest of the party in Benz.
  • Dez arrived as hired help for a small trading company that had come to Benz for the Festival of Shooting Stars.
  • Kirill Amaterasu came on his first solo mission as a Verdant Mendicant, tasked with helping a local farmer's cow give birth.
  • Layla, Vice Captain of the Caelrosa guard, was assigned to Benz to fill a gap in local law enforcement.

Connections

  • Benz
  • Festival of Shooting Stars
  • Queen's Thorns
  • Crash at Benz
  • Massacre at Benz
  • the Thorns
  • Find the Others

Log

Session 2

Canon Fugitive party resupplies at the desert village of Havari; local politics lead them to destroy a cave entrance, inadvertently waking an ancient beast

Session 2

Quick Reference

Session #: 2

Played On:

In-world Date: 9 Pollant 301 Bloom

PCs Present: Calanthe Orchidaceae, Cleke, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Layla

Recap

A read-aloud paragraph: what happened and what it meant. Fleeing the Crown after the Benz incident, the party passed through Havari, a small desert village, to resupply. They got drawn into local politics and, as part of resolving the situation, destroyed the entrance to a cave housing the village's underground spring. The explosion woke an ancient beast that had lain dormant inside. The party collected their supplies and moved on toward Loquat, not knowing what they had set loose.

  • Arrived in Havari and became involved in local politics.
  • Destroyed the entrance to an underground spring cave beneath the village.
  • An ancient beast awakened inside the cave (see Awakening at Havari; the same beast that later struck Loquat).
  • Party gathered supplies and departed for Loquat.

Key Events

The beats worth remembering, as bullets.

Connections

  • Havari
  • Awakening at Havari
  • Loquat

Log

Session 3

Canon Party arrives in Loquat, navigates a Scribes' Union strike and a failing Ascent elevator, is hired by Magister Corbin Locke for a retrieval job at the Envisionarium, and makes first contact with Elara Vane

Session 3

Quick Reference

Session #: 3

Played On:

In-world Date: 11 Pollant 301 Bloom

PCs Present: Calanthe Orchidaceae, Cleke, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Layla

Recap

A read-aloud paragraph: what happened and what it meant. The party entered Loquat through The Roots and landed in the middle of a Scribes' Union strike. The Ascent, the cliff-wall elevator connecting the Roots to The Canopy, was failing with passengers stranded aboard. The party intervened and got everyone down safely. Magister Corbin Locke, Grand Archivist of Loquat, then hired them to retrieve spare parts and blueprints from the Envisionarium. Before the day ended, they also made first contact with Elara Vane.

  • Arrived in Loquat's Roots district.
  • Encountered the Scribes' Union strike in progress.
  • Saved passengers stranded on the failing The Ascent.
  • Magister Corbin Locke hired the party to retrieve parts and blueprints from the Envisionarium.
  • Made first contact with Elara Vane.

Key Events

The beats worth remembering, as bullets.

Connections

  • Loquat
  • The Roots
  • Scribes' Union
  • The Ascent
  • The Canopy
  • Magister Corbin Locke
  • The Envisionarium
  • Elara Vane

Log

Session 4

Canon Party retrieves blueprints from the Envisionarium, clashes with Roots-Agent Nettle, loses Icarly in the escape, and delivers the haul to Locke

Session 4

Quick Reference

Session #: 4

Played On:

In-world Date: 12 Pollant 301 Bloom

PCs Present: Calanthe Orchidaceae, Cleke, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Layla, Spadae

Recap

A read-aloud paragraph: what happened and what it meant. The party entered the haunted Envisionarium and found Alicia meeting Lyra to discuss union plans. Roots-Agent Nettle descended on the site and confronted them. Kirill Amaterasu slipped away with the blueprints while the standoff held Nettle's attention. Nettle then offered the party their freedom in exchange for their divine shard fragments; they refused and fled. Icarly died during the escape. The parts and blueprints were delivered to Magister Corbin Locke as contracted. Dez and Alicia had a heated argument.

Key Events

The beats worth remembering, as bullets.

  • Lyra
  • Roots-Agent Nettle
  • Kirill Amaterasu
  • Icarly
  • Magister Corbin Locke
  • The Envisionarium

Log

Session 5

Canon Disguised party ascends to the Canopy, wins at the Lumen Lottery, and infiltrates the Grand Archives at midnight to swap the Ledger of Divine Artifacts for Elara's fake

Session 5

Quick Reference

Session #: 5

Played On:

In-world Date: 14 Pollant 301 Bloom

PCs Present: Calanthe Orchidaceae, Cleke, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Layla, Spadae

Recap

A read-aloud paragraph: what happened and what it meant. With wanted posters circulating, the party disguised themselves. Magister Corbin Locke warned that the Thorns would arrive at midnight. The party ascended to The Canopy, where Kirill Amaterasu arranged a diagnosis and cure for Calfur. They won big at the Lumen Lottery. At midnight they entered the Grand Archives and located the Ledger of Divine Artifacts, a census tied to Veritas's legacy. They swapped it with a forgery supplied by Elara Vane. An alarm triggered: the fake lacked the original's divine signature.

Key Events

The beats worth remembering, as bullets.

Session 6

Canon Party pushes into the Inner Archives, uncovers three Crown god-consumption operations and the Roots' covert base, escapes undetected, delivers the Ledger to Elara, and flees Loquat before the Thorn task force arrives

Session 6

Quick Reference

Session #: 6

Played On:

In-world Date: 14 Pollant 301 Bloom

PCs Present: Calanthe Orchidaceae, Cleke, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Layla, Spadae

Recap

A read-aloud paragraph: what happened and what it meant. The party pressed deeper into the Inner Archives and discovered a covert Roots base concealed inside. Documents revealed three active Crown operations experimenting on ways to consume gods without Queen Acantha Rosaceae's direct involvement: Alithiel in Cinquefoil, Aeolus in Medlar, and Valerius in Blackthorn. The party escaped without detection, delivered the real Ledger of Divine Artifacts to Elara Vane, and received forged papers, gold, and passage out of Loquat. The main Thorn task force arrived at midnight, after the party was already gone.

Key Events

The beats worth remembering, as bullets.

Log

Session 7

Canon Party discovers Havari and Loquat destroyed by the beast; rendezvous with Elara and Edmund; saves survivors trapped underground while magical runes are lost

Session 7

Quick Reference

Session #: 7

Played On:

In-world Date: 16 Pollant 301 Bloom

PCs Present: Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Calanthe Orchidaceae, Layla, Spadae, Cleke

Recap

A read-aloud paragraph: what happened and what it meant. The party travels toward Blackthorn and finds Havari reduced to rubble. The beast forced its way out of the cave they sealed and destroyed the village before moving on to Loquat. Doubling back, they arrive too late. Loquat is in ruins and the beast is sleeping in the wreckage.

They rendezvous with Elara Vane and Edmund Vane. Elara explains she is looking for a way to extract the divine energy from the Queen. The key lies below Loquat through an entrance the beast's attack opened, and the party is needed to decode magical symbols there. The party declines and instead descends to rescue survivors trapped underground. They save everyone. When the beast wakes, the magical runes are destroyed.

Key Events

The beats worth remembering, as bullets.

Connections

  • Havari
  • Blackthorn
  • Loquat
  • Elara Vane
  • Edmund Vane

Log

Session 8

Canon Beast moves on the Grand Archives; party splits to retrieve an item and slow the creature; Corbin sacrifices his remaining life force to land the killing blow, goes comatose, and is assassinated on 19 Pollant; Val and Vesperae join the party

Session 8

Quick Reference

Session #: 8

Played On:

In-world Date: 17 Pollant 301 Bloom

PCs Present: Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Calanthe Orchidaceae, Layla, Spadae, Cleke, Val, Vesperae Locke

Recap

A read-aloud paragraph: what happened and what it meant. The beast wakes and moves toward the Grand Archives. The party splits: one half races into the Archives' depths to retrieve whatever the beast is after; the other half delays its advance. The retrieval succeeds, though everyone nearly dies.

Magister Corbin Locke appears and burns away his remaining life force to wound the beast mortally. The party delivers the killing blow. Corbin falls into a coma.

Survivors begin rebuilding Loquat. Val and Vesperae Locke formally join the party during this period. On 19 Pollant 301, Corbin is assassinated while still in his coma. Elara Vane dispatches the party to Blackthorn to continue his work.

Key Events

The beats worth remembering, as bullets.

Connections

  • Grand Archives
  • Magister Corbin Locke
  • Loquat
  • Val
  • Vesperae Locke
  • Elara Vane
  • Blackthorn

Log

Session 9

Canon Party travels toward Blackthorn, passes through the desolate village of Gallowfen, briefly rouses a comatose girl named Posy, watches a Crown Levy conscription arrive, and departs for Blackthorn at dawn

Session 9

Quick Reference

Session #: 9

Played On:

In-world Date: 20 Pollant 301 Bloom

PCs Present: Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Calanthe Orchidaceae, Layla, Spadae, Cleke, Val, Vesperae Locke

Recap

A read-aloud paragraph: what happened and what it meant. The party travels toward Blackthorn, passing a roadside shrine without stopping. They clear a Thorn checkpoint without incident.

In Gallowfen, a small and visibly desolate village with hollow storefronts and no young men, they set up a meat stall and briefly draw residents out into the street. They meet Posy, a young girl held in an unexplained coma-like state, and her mother. Using their shards, the party temporarily rouses Posy. She reports dreaming of a large red flower and has no memory of time passing. The coma feels to her like an unbroken sleep.

That evening a Crown conscription force arrives for the Levy. The party watches but does not interfere. Locals also mention a caravan that passed through Gallowfen one to two months ago, traveling from the Capital. The party spends the night and departs the next morning, catching sight of Blackthorn's industrial sprawl from the road as the session ends.

Key Events

The beats worth remembering, as bullets.

  • The conscription unit the party witnessed at Gallowfen is part of a broader Crown practice; see The Levy.

Connections

  • Blackthorn
  • Gallowfen
  • Posy

Log

Session 10

Canon Party arrives in Blackthorn, encounters the city's mysterious wall writings, befriends Cassimir Blackthorn, and Sable begins working her own angle

Session 10

Quick Reference

Session #: 10

Played On:

In-world Date: 22 Pollant 301

PCs Present: Calanthe Orchidaceae, Cleke, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Layla, Spadae, Val, Vesperae Locke

Recap

A read-aloud paragraph: what happened and what it meant. The party entered Blackthorn without difficulty, discarding the false identities Elara Vane had provided. They went to Val's family home and found his mother in a deteriorating mental state; his father and sister had been taken by the Crown Levy.

The next morning the party overheard word of an Aether Golem demonstration scheduled for 26 Pollant 301. Val and Kirill Amaterasu met and befriended Cassimir Blackthorn, younger son of Lord Corvus Blackthorn, who shared research: the Blackthorn Writings that appear and vanish on city walls each day began the same day his brother Theron Blackthorn died, and Cassimir believes they are Theron's communications. Calanthe Orchidaceae and Spadae noticed a woman watching the group. She is Sable, a defected Hive operative posing as infantry sergeant Maret Voss.

As Val and Kirill slipped away from Cassimir's room in Castle Blackthorn, they heard Lord Corvus Blackthorn address his son as 'My Son' for apparently the first time in a long while.

Key Events

The beats worth remembering, as bullets.

Log

Session 11

Canon Edmund Vane reveals the Aether Golem's true purpose; party scouts the training grounds and finds the golem body; Sable recruits Cassimir for a midnight meeting

Session 11

Quick Reference

Session #: 11

Played On:

In-world Date: 24 Pollant 301

PCs Present: Calanthe Orchidaceae, Cleke, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Layla, Spadae, Val, Vesperae Locke

Recap

A read-aloud paragraph: what happened and what it meant. Edmund Vane made contact with the party in the morning and explained that the Aether Golem demonstration on 26 Pollant 301 is not a routine display. The device is built to consume and weaponize divine power, and the Crown intends to use it on the god currently in Blackthorn. The party was tasked with stopping it.

They traveled to the city's central training grounds (the planned demonstration site) and examined the dormant Aether Golem body. They determined it has two components: the golem body draws in divine energy, while the Aether Core (held at Crown Works Installation Seven) focuses and weaponizes it. Spadae noticed an unidentified person who was watching the party and wrote something in a book. This person is not Sable. Kirill Amaterasu disguised himself as a guard, successfully deceiving several soldiers before being recognized later and released. Those guards now know the party infiltrated as guards.

Meanwhile, Sable made contact with Cassimir Blackthorn as Maret Voss and convinced him to meet her at midnight on 24 Pollant 301. The party ended the session planning to infiltrate Crown Works Installation Seven.

Key Events

The beats worth remembering, as bullets.

  • Edmund Vane's briefing on the golem's true purpose connects to The Aether Golem Demonstration.

Connections

  • Edmund Vane
  • Blackthorn
  • Crown Works Installation Seven
  • Sable
  • Cassimir Blackthorn
  • Kirill Amaterasu

Log

Session 12

Canon Session digest from "Session 12, 2026-06-28"

Session 12

Quick Reference

Session #: 12

Played On: 2026-06-28

In-world Date: 24 Pollant 301

PCs Present: Calanthe Orchidaceae, Cleke, Dez, Icarly, Kirill Amaterasu, Layla, Spadae, Val, Vesperae Locke

Recap

A read-aloud paragraph: what happened and what it meant. The party crossed into Crown Works Installation Seven by hugging the loading carts and shimmying along the walls to stay out of the guards' sight lines, with one job: slip past the patrols, push toward the Aether Core, and steal or wreck it before the guard shift changed. Dez ducked east into an abandoned prayer shrine, turned up a ledger of "donations" to a church that should not exist in Elarosea where the Queen is meant to be the only god, pocketed it, and tipped a candle into the dust to set a slow fire as a distraction. In the western cells the group found people sleeping behind bars, and Val recognized the wing as where his family is held. He looted a guard's chest and found his little sister's drawing, "I love you, daddy," confiscated and locked away. Calanthe put a roaming guard to sleep and the body went under the hay.

Dez struck out alone, tried to cross the central chasm along the ceiling beams, slipped, and woke a new power as he fell: flight. A huge cage hung over the chasm, and Dez and Cleke crept up to find a man sitting cross-legged inside who opened glowing eyes and called them "little lambs." The shard on Cleke's bow, a piece of the God of Second Chances handed to them by Alice, lit up in answer and the cage flared green. Uneasy, Dez slept and visited the Dreamcatcher to question Nal'Szomn, who confirmed the caged figure is the war god Valerius and warned that releasing him could cost lives. Kirill argued Valerius was being held so the crown could drain his divinity at the coming demonstration and pushed to free him.

Val took a different path. He burst in on his friend Cassimir Blackthorn and a woman, Maret Voss, who claimed to have worked with Theron Blackthorn and to be escorting Casimir on an audit. Val talked his way in, and they dropped through a trap door into a lab of butchered animals and people, blood across the floor, and a dodecahedron core braced by energy pylons. As her men pried the core loose, Maret told Casimir that the writings reaching him are truly his brother's, that she can bring Theron back by harnessing the caged god (corrected 2026-07-12: she never claimed Theron is alive), and that Casimir's own father signed the death warrant; she told Val that a group called The Hive had been watching him and his friends. Then Roots Arcanist Quill stepped out of the quiet, logged their treason in his book, and called up guards.

The fight turned into a focus-fire on Quill once the party broke free of his Evard's Black Tentacles. Calanthe Orchidaceae slowed him, Vesperae Locke hit him with Sacred Flame and Guiding Bolt, Spadae grappled him and threw him onto a live lightning conduit, Cleke put two arrows and a swarm into him, and after Quill landed a Vitriolic Spear that hit Val for 24 acid, Dez finished him with a Chromatic Orb through the head. Partway through, Kirill Amaterasu's player had to leave and the GM wrote Kirill out safely. Maret and Casimir grabbed the core and bolted ahead, leaving the party to escape on their own with the entrance on fire. Fleeing into the cell blocks, Val found his father and sister drugged into glassy-eyed obedience and led them out, while Dez looked into a cage that had burst open from the inside and found it "not empty." With the normal exit blocked, Dez and Val smashed a hole through a mortared stone wall, and the party led the drugged prisoners out into the night as the session ended. The party infiltrated Crown Works Installation Seven, moving along loading carts and walls to stay out of sight. Dez found an abandoned prayer shrine in the eastern wing, pocketed a Donation Ledger from an unsanctioned church, and set a slow candle fire as a distraction. Val discovered the western cells hold his family and found his sister's confiscated drawing in a guard's chest. Calanthe put a roaming guard to sleep.

Dez crossed the central chasm on overhead beams, slipped, and awakened flight in the fall. He and Cleke found a large cage suspended over the chasm. A man inside sat cross-legged and opened glowing eyes, calling the party 'little lambs.' The shard of the God of Second Chances on Cleke's bow flared green. Dez slept and visited The Dreamcatcher to question Nal'Szomn, who confirmed the prisoner is Valerius and warned that releasing him could cost lives. Kirill argued the Crown was holding Valerius to drain his divinity at the coming demonstration and pushed to free him. The party entered Crown Works Installation Seven by riding and pressing close to the loading carts, slipping past the guard patrols. Val broke in on Cassimir Blackthorn and a woman calling herself Maret Voss, who claimed to be running an audit. Val talked his way in, and the three dropped through a trap door into a laboratory: a blood-soaked floor littered with butchered animals and people, the Aether Core braced in energy pylons. While Sable's men pried the core loose, she told Cassimir that the Blackthorn Writings are his brother's doing, that she can bring Theron Blackthorn back by harnessing the caged god (corrected 2026-07-12: she never claimed he is alive), and that Lord Corvus Blackthorn signed the death warrant. She told Val that The Hive had been watching him and his friends. Then Roots Arcanist Quill stepped from the quiet, logged the treason in his book, and called the guards. The party broke free of his Evard's Black Tentacles and focused on Quill. Calanthe Orchidaceae slowed him; Vesperae Locke hit him with Sacred Flame and Guiding Bolt; Spadae grappled him and threw him onto a live lightning conduit; Cleke put two arrows and a swarm into him. Quill landed a Vitriolic Spear on Val for 24 acid before Dez ended him with a Chromatic Orb through the head. Kirill Amaterasu was written out safely mid-fight. Sable and Cassimir grabbed the core and fled, leaving the party to escape on their own. Fleeing into the cell blocks, Val found his father and sister drugged into glassy-eyed obedience and led them toward the exit. Dez looked into a cage that had burst open from the inside and said it was not empty. With the front exit blocked and the building on fire, Dez and Val smashed through a mortared stone wall and led the prisoners out into the night.

  • Maret Voss tells Cassimir Blackthorn the Blackthorn Writings are truly from his brother, promises she can bring Theron Blackthorn back by harnessing the caged god, and reveals Lord Corvus Blackthorn signed the death warrant (corrected 2026-07-12: she never claimed Theron is alive)
  • Maret Voss tells Val that The Hive has been watching the party
  • Roots Arcanist Quill killed by Dez at Crown Works Installation Seven; see Death of Roots Arcanist Quill
  • Aether Core stolen by Maret Voss and Cassimir Blackthorn; see Theft of the Aether Core
  • Val finds his father and sister drugged in the cell blocks; see Val Finds His Family at Crown Works
  • Dez finds a cage burst open from the inside at Crown Works; contents unknown
  • Kirill Amaterasu written out of the session safely mid-fight

Inside Crown Works Installation Seven, the party spread through restricted corridors. Dez found an abandoned prayer shrine off the east corridor, pocketed the Donation Ledger sitting inside it, then tipped a candlestick into the dust to set a slow fire as a diversion. Layla spent a prepared contingency to have already scouted the western wing and ran back to report cages full of prisoners. Val recognized the block as the one holding his family. He and Calanthe dropped a patrolling guard with a Sleep spell, stashed him under hay, and cracked open a guard's chest, where Val found a child's drawing from his younger sister labeled 'I love you, daddy,' depicting a family of four. Crossing the central chasm by ceiling beams, Dez slipped, fell, and instinctively woke the power of flight, drifting across to the far side. He and Cleke reached a massive cage suspended over the gap. When Dez tapped the bars, the man inside opened glowing eyes and called them 'little lambs.' The shard of the God of Second Chances set into Cleke's bow shone; the cage flared green. Dez covered the shard but the light bled through his fingers. That night Dez slept and traveled to The Dreamcatcher, where Nal'Szomn confirmed the shards are genuine fragments of the God of Second Chances, noted that a god fractured into living people cannot simply be freed, and identified the caged prisoner as Valerius, the Warmind of strategy and war. Nal'Szomn guessed the Queen had arranged his confinement and warned that freeing him could bring casualties depending on how much he liked the people doing it. The answers cost Dez 40 gold.

  • Dez Awakens Flight: Dez slips crossing the central chasm and wakes flight as he falls.
  • Val Finds His Family at Crown Works: Val recognizes the western cell wing as his family's prison block and recovers his sister's drawing.
  • Valerius Found Imprisoned at Crown Works: Dez and Cleke find the war god caged over the chasm; Nal'Szomn confirms his identity.

Two threads converged inside Crown Works Installation Seven. Kirill pushed hard to free Valerius from his cage, arguing the Crown planned to drain the god's divinity at the coming demonstration; Dez resisted. Cleke's Detect Magic confirmed a powerful abjuration ward on the cage, built to hold a god. Valerius said something beneath the cage powered the ward but did not know where. On another path, Val burst in on Cassimir Blackthorn and Maret Voss, who presented the situation as a routine audit escort; Val talked his way into joining them. The group dropped through a trap door hidden under a chair into an underground lab, floors and walls marked by butchered animals and people. The Aether Core sat locked between energy pylons. While her men pried the core free, Maret told Cassimir that the Crown had Theron killed for knowing too much, that the Blackthorn Writings are his brother's voice, that Lord Corvus signed the warrant, and that she can bring Theron back by harnessing the caged god (corrected 2026-07-12: she never claimed he is alive). She told Val that the Hive had been watching the party and intended to deny the Crown its weapon. Roots Arcanist Quill stepped from the shadows, logged this as his ninth entry, charged the group with treason, and summoned three guards. Combat opened: Maret shot Quill with a hidden crossbow; Spadae crit a veteran with a thrown gourd; Cleke killed a second veteran and called fairy birds; Calanthe heated a guard's armor red-hot; Vesperae cast Moonbeam on Quill. Quill countered with Evard's Black Tentacles, restraining Calanthe, then a Fireball dropping her to 11 hp. The party broke free of the tentacles.

  • 24 Pollant 301: Vault combat rounds two and three. Calanthe Orchidaceae, freed from the tentacles and dropping Heat Metal concentration, cast Slow on Roots Arcanist Quill and a Crown Vault Guard (Veteran); both failed and were slowed. Val raged and called on divine fury against a warrior veteran. Kirill Amaterasu killed a badly wounded Crown soldier (at 15 of 65 HP) with two punches, missed both flurry strikes on a second veteran, took 7 damage in return, then was pulled safely from the fight when his player had to leave. Vesperae Locke targeted Quill with Sacred Flame (he saved), then landed Guiding Bolt for 14 damage, marking him for advantage on the next attack. Spadae hit Quill with greatsword and sneak attack for 10 damage. Dez flew down in front of Quill and cast Fear, telling him his path had led him here to die; Quill resisted, but a slowed Crown soldier failed and was frightened. Meanwhile Maret Voss's three crew members unsealed the central housing and handed her the Aether Core; she and Cassimir Blackthorn fled, warning the ship changeover was soon. Fire above had tripped the facility alarm. Val cleaved the slowed warrior veteran in two and chose to stay and finish Quill rather than chase the core.
  • Spadae impaled Quill on a lightning conduit; Cleke, Calanthe, and Dez wore him down; Quill's Vitriolic Spear damaged Val (24 acid + 5d4 ongoing), Spadae, and Dez before Dez killed him with a Chromatic Orb. See Death of Roots Arcanist Quill.
  • The party climbed out of the vault to find Maret Voss and Cassimir Blackthorn already gone with the Aether Core. See Theft of the Aether Core.
  • The entrance was on fire; fleeing into the cell blocks, the party found rows of prisoners awake but drugged into unresponsive obedience; unlocking doors did nothing.
  • Val found his father and sister among the prisoners, glassy-eyed and unrecognizing. See Val Finds His Family at Crown Works.
  • Dez found a cage burst open from the inside and reported it "not empty."
  • Dez and Val breached a mortared wall (combined check 54); Dez used Control Flames on a candle to lead the drugged prisoners out through the gap.

Session 13

Canon Party escapes Crown Works with the drugged prisoners; Edmund Vane names Marett Voss as Sable and confirms Theron is long dead; Sable pitches the party at the training grounds and gives them until evening to choose a side.

Session 13

Quick Reference

Session #: 13

Played On: 2026-07-12

In-world Date: 24 Pollant 301 Bloom

PCs Present: Val, Kirill Amaterasu, Calanthe Orchidaceae, Dez, Vesperae Locke, Spadae

Recap

A read-aloud paragraph: what happened and what it meant. Fresh from Crown Works Installation Seven, the party woke in a rubble-choked alley in Blackthorn with a dozen drugged prisoners in tow, among them Val's father and his 15-year-old sister. The installation burned behind them and the city bells rang out a search. Guided by Kirill Amaterasu's rooftop scouting and Calanthe Orchidaceae's read of the guard patrols, they slipped the net street by street, put a wandering guard to sleep before a conditioned prisoner could give them away, and reached the door of their Hive contact Edmund Vane.

Inside, Edmund pried the story out of them and named their new ally. "Marett Voss" is Sable, a former Hive operative who defected a month or two ago. She learned the Hive's true goal, to save the Queen, and rejected it: kill the tyrant instead, fight a god with a god. She already holds half of what that takes, the stolen Aether Core, and the other half, the Aether Golem, still sits in Blackthorn. Edmund also insisted that Cassimir Blackthorn's brother Theron Blackthorn died long ago at the hands of The Roots, which makes Sable's promise to resurrect him a lie she is feeding the boy.

Then Casimir himself knocked, using the secret Hive code Sable had taught him. Val and Calanthe met him at the door in the rain, where he begged for their help and produced a never-sent letter in Theron's hand as proof Sable knew his brother. He asked them to come to the training grounds at noon, warned them to be wary of the Hive, and ran off. Meanwhile Dez and Kirill slipped out the back to guard the Golem, spotted a scarred man watching Casimir from a rooftop, tailed him across the city, and were shot out of the sky by crossbows and hauled off by Sable's agents.

The two woke bound in a stone cellar, freed themselves with Kirill's otters and Dez's earth-shaping, and rejoined the others by morning. After failing to find any way to cure the prisoners of the drug, warm weed, the party left them at the now-compromised safe house and Edmund walked out alone. At the training grounds, dressed for a demonstration that its crown handlers do not yet know is dead, Sable found them. She made her pitch one character at a time, naming each one's grievance against the Queen, and gave them until evening to choose a side. As Casimir arrived, Sable pressed Kirill to "see reason," told him she would see him there that night, and walked through the doors as the guards stepped aside for her. The guards closed ranks again behind her, and the session ended with Kirill and Dez left outside the sealed doors, the choice still unmade.

Key Events

The beats worth remembering, as bullets.

  • The party regained their senses in a Blackthorn alley beside a dozen freed prisoners drugged into obedience, including Val's father (a tanner) and his 15-year-old sister, with the burning Crown Works Installation Seven at their backs and the bells ringing a search; their goal was to reach Edmund's safe house.
  • Kirill Amaterasu scouted ahead on a stealth run, slipped past a pair of lantern-carrying guards, and waved the group through for the escape's first success.
  • Kirill then took to the rooftops with acrobatic parkour to map a route, but the run stalled on a near miss just as a guard stepped out of a doorway beside the hidden party.
  • A conditioned prisoner began drifting toward the guard's lantern, and Calanthe Orchidaceae's Sleep spell dropped the guard where he stood before the girl could expose them.
  • At a shuttered market, Calanthe read the guard patrol pattern from cover and threaded the whole group through unseen, reaching the spot where they last met their contact.
  • Unable to remember how to signal him, they searched the dark until a door cracked open and Edmund Vane waved them into the safe house.
  • Edmund questioned them and learned they had released a god, burned down the installation, and handed the stolen Aether Core to a new "contact."
  • Edmund revealed that "Marett Voss" is Sable, a former inner-circle operative of The Hive who defected a month or two ago because she rejected the Hive's goal of saving the Queen Acantha Rosaceae; Sable means to kill the Queen using a god against a god.
  • Edmund warned that Sable now holds half of what it takes to kill a god (the Aether Core) and that the other half, the Aether Golem, still sits in Blackthorn, which set the party talking about racing her to it.
  • On the boy, Edmund insisted Theron Blackthorn died long ago, taken off the field by The Roots and confirmed dead within the week, so Sable's resurrection promise to Cassimir Blackthorn is a lie meant to use him for access.
  • A three-then-one knock came at the door in the Hive's secret code; Edmund saw Casimir through the spyhole and warned it made no sense for the boy to know the knock unless Sable had taught it to him.
  • The party split: Dez and Kirill left by the back trap door toward the Aether Golem in case Casimir was a decoy, while Calanthe and Val handled Casimir and Vesperae Locke and Spadae stayed with the prisoners.
  • Calanthe and Val opened the door to Casimir, who begged their help to bring Theron back and showed a never-sent letter in Theron's own hand, given to him by Sable as proof she knew his brother.
  • Casimir admitted he did not know the full plan, said Sable had told him where to find them and taught him the knock, asked them to come to the training grounds at noon, and warned that the Hive is "not all it turns out to be" before hugging them and running off.
  • Behind the safe house, Dez and Kirill spotted a figure on a rooftop watching Casimir; Kirill pinned him as a clean-shaven man of about 48 with a scar across his face and a cloak built to blend into the dark, who jotted a note and slipped away.
  • Dez flew after the scarred man carrying Kirill, tailing him across Blackthorn to a building in another district, where four crossbow bolts tore them both out of the air; they fell unconscious and Sable's agents carried them off.
  • Back inside, Calanthe told the group everything: Sable knows the safe house, taught Casimir the knock, and has promised to resurrect his brother.
  • Val identified the prisoners' drug as warm weed, which numbs the mind and makes users obey, and flagged it as strange that his young sister had been dosed since the drug is not normally given to women.
  • After weighing single-target cures, a rehab center, and the risk that the escaped prisoners are being hunted by the Blackthorn Knights, the party decided they could not cure the dozen and left them at the safe house, trusting Sable would not turn the Knights loose on innocents.
  • Judging the safe house compromised and refusing to be responsible for the prisoners, Edmund wished them luck and left out the back.
  • Dez and Kirill woke bound and blindfolded in a stone cellar; Kirill's summoned otters slipped their manacles and Dez shaped the earth to open the ceiling, and they climbed out into a foundry district by mid-morning.
  • The pair returned to the safe house, debriefed the others, and Vesperae's Prayer of Healing restored them to full HP.
  • At the training grounds the party found the yard being chalked and staged for the Aether Golem demonstration the next day, guarded twice over, its handlers unaware the core was stolen and the god had escaped.
  • Vesperae, disguised as an art dealer from Cinquefoil, charmed a local named Tim for information and confirmed Casimir was present and openly throwing his weight around.
  • Before the party could split up, Sable (still as Marett Voss) intercepted them, revealed she knew they worked with the Hive, and pitched joining her to kill the Queen with the stolen machine rather than "play goody-two-shoes."
  • Confronted by Dez over the crossbow ambush, Sable said her agents had fired because they could not tell who was tailing one of her operatives in the dark, and that had she wanted the two dead, they would be.
  • Sable dismissed Elara Vane's Hive as all patience and no action, called the plan to save the Queen no plan at all, and argued the machine built to eat gods should be turned back on the crown.
  • Calanthe refused to be swayed, saying the party frees gods from torment and exists to protect people; Sable answered by working each character's grievance in turn, naming the Queen's harm to Val's family, Vesperae's father, Spade's uncle, and Kirill's Verdant Mendicants, and citing conditions in Loquat and the Benz incident.
  • Sable belittled the Florescence Calanthe prizes, gave the party until evening to bring an answer, and stepped back as Casimir arrived, assuring them the boy acts of his own free will.
  • Pressed by Sable for a decision, Kirill told her he was not sure he would do what she had asked of him.
  • Sable urged Kirill to "see reason" and said she would see him at the training ground that night.
  • Sable walked through the doors into the training ground as the guards parted for her, then resumed their posts and blocked the entrance behind her.
  • The session ended with Kirill and Dez left outside the sealed doors, a choice still in front of them.

Session 14

Canon Valerius attacks the training grounds; Spadae pilots the Aether Golem to consume him; the party splits when Spadae sides with Sable.

Session 14

Quick Reference

Session #: 14

Played On: 2026-07-26

In-world Date: 25 Pollant 301 Bloom

PCs Present: Calanthe Orchidaceae, Kirill Amaterasu, Dez, Spadae

Recap

The session opened outside Blackthorn's training grounds with Sable's deadline still in force. Rather than pick blind, Calanthe Orchidaceae put the party to sleep and walked them into The Dreamcatcher, where Nal'Szomn sold truthful answers at a price. They learned that Sable cannot bring Theron Blackthorn back: the only power over the dead is the God of Death, and Queen Acantha Rosaceae has already consumed him. Kirill Amaterasu pressed for a way to save Calfur and came away with a ritual location: The Index, home of Veritas and the place where the Queen first sealed a god into herself. When asked whether to kill the Queen, Nal'Szomn offered only that "the line between naivety and hopelessness is almost invisible." The party bought healing potions, a wand of fireballs, and a scroll of Zone of Truth before waking.

At night they slipped into the quiet training grounds and cornered Sable and Cassimir Blackthorn beside The Aether Golem. Calanthe cast Zone of Truth over the whole group. Under it, Sable admitted she never worked with Theron, only admired him and hunted down how he died: the Queen killed him, and Lord Corvus Blackthorn's inaction let it happen. She held to her promise to return Theron but refused to say how. The confrontation backfired socially: Cassimir came out trusting Sable, who stayed calm and apologized, and resenting his friends who had come in swinging. Stalled by Sable's ultimatum outside the Blackthorn Training Grounds, the party could not agree on the Queen's fate. Calanthe cast Sleep and pulled them into The Dreamcatcher, where the dream-merchant Nal'Szomn sold truthful answers for story points. He confirmed that only the god of death can touch the dead, and Queen Acantha Rosaceae has consumed him. Sable's promise to Cassimir Blackthorn is a lie she knows is a lie. Armed with this, the party agreed to help. Before they could begin, Valerius walked in through the north gate and seized the guards' minds. What followed was a scramble: Spadae climbed inside The Aether Golem and drove it by raw muscle, Dez arrived late and tore a guard apart with Vampiric Touch, and Calanthe was dropped mid-fight and revived with a potion. On the last round Sable fed The Aether Core to Spadae, the Golem lit up, and Valerius was pulled inside. After the fight the party fractured. Calanthe, Kirill, and Dez refused to follow Sable and said so plainly. Spadae sided with her and departed with Sable, Cassimir, and the Golem. Sable left the three who refused a Silver Emblem to summon her if they changed their minds. Walking back to the safe house through waking city alarms, Calanthe spotted a watcher on the same rooftop the scarred tail had used the night before. A bird landed on his shoulder and he slipped away. The party visited The Dreamcatcher and spent their points with Nal'Szomn. Kirill Amaterasu asked about curing Calfur and learned that the nascent god inside feeds on the calf's life force and will kill him at birth, and that no method exists to release a divinity outright. Any ritual to consume or transfer a divinity must be performed at The Index, home of Veritas and the site where Queen Acantha Rosaceae first sealed a god into herself. Calanthe Orchidaceae pressed the merchant on killing the Queen; he declined, saying only that the line between naivety and hopelessness is almost invisible. Spadae took that as a yes. The party bought five Greater Healing potions, one Superior Healing potion, a wand of fireballs for Spadae, and a scroll of Zone of Truth before waking. That night the party slipped into the training grounds and found Sable and Cassimir Blackthorn beside The Aether Golem. Calanthe cast Zone of Truth from the scroll, catching everyone in the circle and tripping alarms. Under the effect, Sable admitted she had never served under Theron Blackthorn but had admired him; she traced his death to the Queen and named Lord Corvus Blackthorn's inaction as part of the cause. The party pressed because Edmund Vane had already told them the story she fed Val was false. She still swore she intends and is able to return Theron to Cassimir, but would not say how. Sable answered calmly and apologized. Cassimir came out trusting Sable over the party and resenting the ambush. Pushed by Spadae, the party agreed to help. Her guards began hauling the Aether Golem toward its launch point. Key events: Party Visits the Dreamcatcher, Sable Questioned Under Zone of Truth, Party Splits Over Sable Valerius arrived at Blackthorn Training Grounds on the morning of 25 Pollant, frightening the party with a failed Wisdom save and mind-controlling Sable's dozen guards into attacking. Sable broke for The Aether Golem immediately. The party stalled roughly six rounds: Spadae climbed inside the Golem and piloted it by brute strength, casting fireball from within; Dez arrived late and killed a guard with Vampiric Touch; Sable repeatedly healed the party mid-fight. Cassimir Blackthorn was knocked unconscious and later fled to safety. Calanthe Orchidaceae dropped to death saves before Dez force-fed her a Superior Healing potion from her own bag. On the final round Sable threw The Aether Core to Spadae, who slotted it in; the Golem powered up and consumed Valerius, freeing the surviving guards. Afterward Calanthe, Kirill Amaterasu, and Dez refused Sable's invitation to her base and denounced her. Spadae sided with Sable and departed with her, Cassimir, and the god-filled Golem. Sable gave the three refusers a Silver Emblem that summons her when pressed into the floor. Returning to the Hive safe house, Calanthe spotted a watcher on the same rooftop the scarred tail had used the night before; a bird landed on his shoulder before he slipped away. The safe house was untouched.

Session 15

Canon Party rests at the compromised Blackthorn safe house, forces Elara to stop operating them blind, relocates by rail car to a hidden forest base near Cinquefoil, Elara reveals her immortal origin and the Hive's true goal, and Corvus and Kefra join as new allies.

Session 15

Quick Reference

Session #: 15

Played On: 2026-08-16

In-world Date: 25 Pollant 301 Bloom

PCs Present: Val, Calanthe Orchidaceae, Vesperae Locke, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu

Recap

The party bedded down at the compromised Blackthorn safe house alongside Val's drugged family and roughly a dozen other captives freed from Crown Works Installation Seven. Near midnight a storm woke Calanthe Orchidaceae, and through the wall she caught Edmund Vane arguing with Elara Vane. Edmund pushed Elara to stop sending the party in blind after one of them nearly died, warning she would lose them the way she lost Sable. Calanthe stepped out and pressed the same point: the group had already lost two of their own, one to death and Spadae to Sable's side. Elara conceded, declared the safe house compromised, and ordered Edmund to move everyone to a new base.

At dawn Val carried his sister and father to his mother in the Blackthorn slums. Her sanity and hope returned at the sight of them. The family shared a last meal before Val left to continue the mission. His mother directed the party to a conscription-victim support group founded by the late Lady of Blackthorn, and that group took in the remaining captives. Vesperae Locke used twilight magic to leave Val's mother with warmth and reassurance before they departed.

Edmund led the party out of Blackthorn by abandoned rail car, northeast to a hidden forest base Elara had built about a day outside Cinquefoil. Once they left the city, Calfur's condition worsened: Veritas was no longer loose in Blackthorn to suppress the divine being incubating inside it.

In the base's planning room Elara told the party the truth. She died of the Grey Wilt centuries ago and was written back to life, and left immortal, by Queen Acantha Rosaceae using Veritas's ledger, the same book the party stole in the Grand Archives Heist. Acantha, ignored by the gods during the plague, descended beneath Caelrosa, consumed Veritas, and waged war on the gods that had failed her people until the stolen power drove her mad. The Hive's true goal is to free Acantha from that madness, not kill her. Elara assigned the party to infiltrate Cinquefoil and stop the god-extraction there before Sable arrives. Edmund brought in Lord Corvus Blackthorn, turned against Sable by Cassimir's defection, along with Corvus's white-clad barbarian aid Kefra Chico. The party leveled to 6.

Key Events

  • At the compromised Blackthorn safe house, the party long-rested among Val's drugged family and roughly a dozen captives freed from the Crown Works Installation Seven.
  • Near midnight Calanthe Orchidaceae woke and overheard Edmund Vane arguing with Elara Vane in the next room, with Edmund demanding she stop sending the party in blind after one of them nearly died.
  • Edmund warned Elara that Sable's arguments had landed, and that she would lose the party the way she lost Sable unless she told them what they fought for; Elara said only that she would think about it.
  • Calanthe revealed herself and told Elara the party had already lost two of their own, one to death and Spadae to Sable's side, and persuaded her that they could not keep working blind.
  • Elara conceded, declared the safe house compromised, and ordered Edmund Vane to move everyone to a new base, promising to find a home for the drugged captives once she had time.
  • Val chose to bring only his sister and father to safety and leave the other captives to the party.
  • At dawn Val returned Val's Sister and Val's Father to his mother in the Blackthorn slums, where her sanity and hope returned and the family shared a last meal before he left.
  • Val refused his mother's parting gift of ten gold, then persuaded her to keep it, and Vesperae Locke used twilight magic to leave her with warmth and reassurance.
  • Val's mother directed the party to a support group that rehabilitates conscription victims, founded by the late Lady of Blackthorn (wife of Lord Corvus Blackthorn, mother of Cassimir Blackthorn and Theron Blackthorn); the group took in the dozen drugged captives.
  • Edmund led the party out of Blackthorn on an abandoned rail car, northeast to a hidden forest base Elara had built about a day outside Cinquefoil.
  • Once they left Blackthorn, Calfur's condition worsened, because Veritas was no longer loose in the city to hold back the divine being incubating inside it.
  • In the planning room, Elara revealed she had died of the The Grey Wilt centuries ago and was written back to life, and left immortal, by Queen Acantha Rosaceae using Veritas's ledger, the same book the party stole in the Grand Archives Heist (Resurrection of Elara Aurum).
  • Elara revealed that Acantha, ignored by the gods during the Grey Wilt, went beneath Caelrosa, defeated and consumed Veritas the God of Truth (Consumption of Veritas), then waged war on every god that failed her people, and that the stolen power drove her mad.
  • Elara named The Hive's true goal: to free Acantha from the divine power binding her sanity, because she believes the Queen deserves a second chance rather than Sable's death sentence.
  • Elara explained Acantha went into seclusion about five years ago and set agents in god-holding city-states to extract their gods for her; the agents succeeded in Blackthorn, and Cinquefoil is next.
  • Elara assigned the party to infiltrate Cinquefoil, stop the god-extraction there, and reach it before Sable does.
  • Edmund brought in Lord Corvus Blackthorn as a new ally, turned against Sable by Cassimir's defection, along with Corvus's aid, a white-clad barbarian named Kefra Chico, who joined the party.
  • Edmund told Dez that the Crown Works was staffed by the Crown rather than by Blackthorn, and left unclear whether Corvus knew of the party's hand in burning it down.
  • The party leveled up to level 6 before the session wrapped.

Log

  • 25 Pollant 301: Near midnight, Calanthe Orchidaceae overheard Edmund Vane arguing with Elara Vane at the Blackthorn safe house; Calanthe intervened, cited the loss of a party member and Spadae to Sable's side, and forced Elara's concession; Elara declared the safe house compromised and ordered relocation (Safe House Midnight Confrontation).

  • 27 Pollant 301: Lord Corvus Blackthorn and his aid Kefra Chico joined the party at the forest base as allies for the Cinquefoil mission.
  • 27 Pollant 301: All party members leveled to 6.

Session 16

Canon Party enters Cinquefoil on Petal Rest under warrant; Calanthe returns to House Potentilla; Aeneas Solanaceae reveals a false engagement claim; Calfur begins recovering at the manor.

Session 16

Quick Reference

Session #: 16

Played On: 2026-09-27

In-world Date: 1 Caducan 301 Bloom

PCs Present: Val, Calanthe Orchidaceae, Vesperae Locke, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Kefra Chico, Cleke

Recap

The party rode into Cinquefoil on the first day of Petal Rest, Lord Corvus Blackthorn riding with them and vouching them through a gate where Calanthe Orchidaceae's warrant hung for public view. Val clung to the carriage underside, Kirill posed as Kefra Chico's court jester, Vesperae wore a veil, and Calanthe feigned sleep against Corvus's shoulder. The guard waved them through. Inside, Corvus split off for his own business, leaving Kefra with the group.

In the Artist Quarter, Charybdis and Charlotte were running a print stall and already held warrant prints of every party member except Kefra. Charlotte warned them not to linger. The route to House Potentilla went over the garden wall by Calanthe's childhood path. Cleke fell mid-climb and drew a guard who recognized Calanthe, who walked the group to the front door instead. The manor guard had doubled since she left.

Lord Vanda Potentilla wept at Calanthe's return. Lady Cattelya Potentilla spoke as if she had never been gone, asking when she would finish her Florescence studies. Vanda recognized Vesperae Locke as Ms. Locke and extended his protection to the whole party despite knowing about their warrants, then disclosed Cattelya's secret work: a memorial to The Grey Wilt, commissioned by the Queen (The Queen's Commission). In the guest room, the party confirmed their task was to stop the Crown's god-consumption plot in Cinquefoil, acknowledged they had no leads yet, and reminded themselves they still needed to find Cassimir Blackthorn. Cleke identified a name copied from the Inner Archives as Alithiel, the Keeper of Lost Things, and the group established the sleeping city god as Amorphia, god of beauty, splendor, glory, and the arts.

At the Petal Rest carnival, Charybdis ran a prediction market and Charlotte watched the crowd, using the party as bait for any hunters. Cleke won the archery game. Vesperae bet Charybdis fifty gold that a fight would break out, and Val launched Kirill about fifteen feet in a staged brawl that won her the wager. At the fountain jousting, both Val and Kirill backed the baker's boy, who beat the nine-year champion and doubled their bets. Festival masks at sundown gave the party cover to return to the manor for dinner.

Dinner brought two surprises. First, Magister Ilex Verbena, visiting from the capital to help Lady Cattelya with the commission, joined the table. Then Aeneas Solanaceae, heir to House Solanaceae, arrived and greeted Calanthe as his fiance, a claim she had never agreed to. She pulled him outside and learned he had told her parents they were together after she vanished, taken work from Lord Vanda, and settled in as the household's future lord. She called him a liar. He forgave her and left before dinner ended. The night closed with Calanthe reuniting with her old lady-in-waiting Althea, and with Kirill's medicine check finding Calfur sleeping soundly and finally recovering after weeks of decline on the road.

Key Events

  • The party set out from their forest base outside Cinquefoil by railway and carriage on the first of Caducan, the opening day of Petal Rest, with Lord Corvus Blackthorn riding along and Kefra Chico left in his charge.
  • At the Cinquefoil gate the party found a carefully painted warrant of Calanthe Orchidaceae hung for public view, labeled treason, sedition, and arson.
  • To clear the gate inspection, Val clung to the underside of the carriage, Kirill posed as Kefra Chico's court jester, Vesperae wore a veil, and Calanthe feigned sleep against Corvus's shoulder with her face hidden.
  • Lord Corvus Blackthorn vouched for the group and the deferential guard waved the carriage through without a real search, then Corvus split off for separate business in the city.
  • In the Artist Quarter, Calanthe reunited with the traveling pair Charybdis and Charlotte, who were running a print stall and already held warrant prints of every party member except Kefra Chico; Charlotte warned them not to stay in one place too long.
  • The party climbed the east garden wall of House Potentilla by Calanthe's old childhood route, but Cleke's wings failed and he fell, the noise drew a guard who recognized Calanthe, and he walked them to the front door; Calanthe noticed the manor guard had doubled since she left.
  • At the door Lord Vanda Potentilla embraced Calanthe in tears, while Lady Cattelya greeted her as if she had never been gone and pressed her about finishing her Florescence studies.
  • Lord Vanda recognized Vesperae as Ms. Locke, granted the whole party his protection inside the manor despite knowing of their warrants, and revealed Lady Cattelya's secret work: a memorial to The Grey Wilt commissioned by the Queen (The Queen's Commission).
  • Debriefing in the guest room, the party confirmed their task in Cinquefoil was to find and stop the Crown's plot to consume the city's sleeping god, agreed they had no leads yet, and reminded themselves they still need to find Cassimir Blackthorn.
  • Cleke raised a name he had copied from the Inner Archives, "Alithiel, Keeper of Lost Things" (Alithiel), while the god of Cinquefoil was established as Amorphia, god of beauty, splendor, glory, and the arts.
  • The party judged the Potentillas were probably kept in the dark like Blackthorn had been, decided to use the festival to scope the city, and Calanthe made everyone costumes in her old manor workshop.
  • At the Petal Rest carnival, Charybdis ran a prediction market on the games while Charlotte watched the crowd, using the party as bait to draw out anyone hunting them.
  • Cleke won the petal shot archery game with the stall's floppy old bow.
  • Vesperae bet Charybdis fifty gold that a fight would break out, and Val immediately punched Kirill and launched him about fifteen feet in a staged brawl that won the wager.
  • At the fountain jousting, Val and Kirill both backed the baker's boy, who beat the sixty-year-old stone carter and nine-year champion, doubling their bets.
  • At sundown the whole square donned festival masks, giving the party anonymity to return to the manor for dinner.
  • Dinner introduced Magister Ilex Verbena, visiting from the capital to help Lady Cattelya with the Grey Wilt commission and staying at the manor for weeks.
  • Aeneas Solanaceae, heir to House Solanaceae, arrived and greeted Calanthe as his fiance, a relationship she had no memory of agreeing to.
  • Calanthe pulled Aeneas outside and learned he had told her parents they were together after she went missing, taken work from Lord Vanda, and settled into the household as the future lord of Cinquefoil; she called him a liar, he deflected and forgave her, then left before dinner ended.
  • In her preserved bedroom, Calanthe reunited with Althea, her mother's lady-in-waiting who had raised her, and Althea offered to look after her again.
  • Kirill's medicine check showed Calfur, sick and worsening every day of the journey, sleeping soundly and recovering for the first time at House Potentilla.

Session 17

Canon Second day of Petal Rest at House Potentilla: Cattelya forgets Calanthe's homecoming, warded guards in city green and missing Wilt books, Verbena unmasked as a Queen's Thorn, her orchard offer, Vanda's confession, and the memorial draft's face cut out after the masque.

Session 17

Quick Reference

Session #: 17

Played On: 2026-10-04

In-world Date: 2 Caducan 301 Bloom (second day of Petal Rest)

PCs Present: Calanthe Orchidaceae, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Cleke

Recap

The second day of Petal Rest, 2 Caducan 301 Bloom, at Potentilla Manor in Cinquefoil. Lady Cattelya Potentilla woke Calanthe Orchidaceae with a breakfast tray and no memory of her homecoming or their dinner (Lady Cattelya Forgets Calanthe's Homecoming). Althea blamed the strain of The Queen's Commission. Lord Vanda Potentilla was evasive at a guarded terrace breakfast while Calfur ate heartily under the table.

The party searched the house (Party Investigates the Potentilla Manor). Silent, regimented guards in city green stood beside the house guards, each with an identical faint abjuration aura behind the lapel that Cleke found with Detect Magic. The Wilt histories and the nursery songbook with the Wilt's lament were gone from the library. The maid Bryony told them of Cattelya's lapses, showed them name bread baked weeks early with one name pressed twice, and pointed them to a listening grill off the linen passage. Through that grill Calanthe recognized Magister Verbena's Grey Ledger-Book as the kind Quill carried at Crown Works Installation Seven, which proved Magister Ilex Verbena a Queen's Thorn (Verbena Unmasked as a Queen's Thorn).

In the orange orchard Verbena named each of the party from her book, said she had reported none of them, and offered safety under the Potentilla roof if they left Cattelya's sessions alone. She admitted the hunt for "the one face the Queen needs" costs the lady memories (Verbena's Offer in the Orange Orchard). Dez privately threatened to kill Cattelya and backed down. In his study Vanda confessed that the Crown coerced House Potentilla under threat of a garrison, that every lesson strips Cattelya's memories, and that Verbena hunts a person inside the house (Lord Vanda's Confession in the Study). The party chose to hold its cards.

Late after the masque the workroom stood open and uncovered. Cattelya's memorial to The Grey Wilt filled six tables, hundreds of portraits around one faceless woman, and on the newest draft the face had been cut out with a knife and taken (The Face Cut from the Memorial Draft).

Key Events

  • On the morning of the second day of Petal Rest, Lady Cattelya Potentilla woke Calanthe Orchidaceae in her old bedroom at the Potentilla manor in Cinquefoil. Cattelya did not remember Calanthe arriving the day before or their dinner together, then hurried off to "speak with your father."
  • Althea, Calanthe's new lady-in-waiting, told Calanthe that Lady Cattelya had been confused like this for about a week and blamed the strain of The Queen's Commission. Calanthe noted that stress had never erased such recent memories before.
  • Calanthe woke Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, and Cleke in the guest room, where Calfur was already up, lively, and hungry. Kirill had kept Calfur in the guest room overnight.
  • At breakfast on the manor's second terrace, Lord Vanda Potentilla ate alone with the party. Lady Cattelya and Magister Ilex Verbena had eaten hours earlier and gone to their morning session. House guards at the terrace faced inward toward the table, and a house-high scaffold covered in sailcloth stood in Cinquefoil's Great Square below. Aeneas Solanaceae was not at breakfast.
  • Calfur ate heartily under the breakfast table. This stood out, because Calfur normally loses his appetite when he is ill. Dez tried to examine him and noticed nothing.
  • Lord Vanda Potentilla told the party they had the run of the gardens and library. He added that Magister Ilex Verbena had borrowed most of the library's books on the Wilt for the commission.
  • Calanthe Orchidaceae asked Lord Vanda Potentilla about the commission and about Althea, who had been her mother's lady-in-waiting before being assigned to Calanthe. Vanda said Calanthe was nearing the age to learn the Potentilla trade. Calanthe's insight (19) showed he was holding something back and glancing at the guards, so she dropped the subject.
  • Calanthe Orchidaceae noticed (perception 18) that the Potentilla manor had far more guards than usual. Alongside the familiar house guards were unfamiliar guards in Cinquefoil city green who stood in a rigid, regimented way, unlike Cinquefoil's relaxed city watch.
  • In the guest room, Calanthe Orchidaceae listed her suspicions to the party: the strange guards, her parents' odd behavior, and Aeneas Solanaceae claiming to be her fiance. The DM explained that guards across Elarosea get the same base training, but Blackthorn guards carry themselves in a far more regimented way than Cinquefoil guards.
  • Dez and Cleke walked past the guards to test them. The house guards greeted the party warmly. The city-uniformed guards only nodded and would not speak.
  • Cleke cast Detect Magic and, with an Arcana result of 15, found that every extra city-uniformed guard at the Potentilla manor carried an identical faint abjuration aura from something behind their lapel. The house staff had no such aura.
  • In the Potentilla library, Calanthe Orchidaceae confirmed that the Wilt history books were gone. She also recognized that an old nursery songbook containing the Wilt's lament was missing ("when the wilt came up the water stairs and the high town barred his door, who sat with the low town through the night..."). Calanthe took three easy-reading books for herself.
  • Calanthe Orchidaceae explained The Grey Wilt to Cleke: a plague and catastrophe about 300 years ago that Queen Acantha Rosaceae ended before founding The Acanthine era.
  • Outside the Potentilla workroom, Calanthe Orchidaceae overheard Magister Ilex Verbena lecturing about the Wilt. Calanthe got a tea tray from a young maid in the kitchen and brought it in. Verbena said the work was going well but the deadline was close and another session might be needed that day. Calanthe saw covered drafts on a table and a makeshift classroom with a desk and chalkboard.
  • At the midday meal, Magister Ilex Verbena arrived with a grey book under her arm and asked Lord Vanda Potentilla for another session with Lady Cattelya at the third bell after noon. She said the unveiling date would be announced the next night and that "her ladyship has not yet heard the whole of the Wilt." She told the party she would find them after the session.
  • Lord Vanda Potentilla reminded the party that another masquerade was being held in Cinquefoil that night and that they should wear masks. He also said Althea was always nearby if Calanthe needed her.
  • In the Potentilla kitchen, a young maid named Bryony told the party that Lady Cattelya had called her by her mother's name, though her mother had been dead nine years. The lapses began about three weeks earlier. Each day Cattelya came out of the lessons exhausted and rested for the rest of the day.
  • Bryony showed the party a stale piece of Petal Rest name bread that Lady Cattelya had baked weeks too early. One name was pressed into it twice, which suggested another dead family member's name had been left off. Bryony then told them about a grill behind the panelling in a room off the linen passage where they could overhear the lessons. Calanthe asked her to keep the matter within the family.
  • From the grill in a narrow passage behind the Potentilla manor's East Wing, the party watched Magister Ilex Verbena tell Lady Cattelya how the Wilt came up the water stairs. Verbena held a grey cloth book with brass corners and red-ruled ledger columns, and her pen wrote in it without her looking. Lady Cattelya sat doodling in her notes, as she often does.
  • Calanthe Orchidaceae (history with advantage) recognized Verbena's book as the same kind Quill, Crown Arcanist carried at Crown Works Installation Seven. The Queen's Thorns and Queen's Roots use these books. The party now knew Magister Ilex Verbena was a Queen's Thorn, not an ordinary government official.
  • A servant summoned the party to the orange orchard on the Potentilla manor's fourth terrace. There Magister Ilex Verbena touched her grey book once for each of them and named them: a monk from the Southern Forests, a sorcerer from the roots of Loquat, an archer who cannot see what he shoots, and the daughter of the house. She said their wanted posters were good likenesses and that she had reported none of this to the garrison.
  • Magister Ilex Verbena offered the party a deal: leave Lady Cattelya's sessions undisturbed, and they could sleep safely under the Potentilla roof while the garrison never heard of them. She said the Queen wants an accurate memorial. Somewhere in Lady Cattelya's memories is "the one face the Queen needs," which the lady does not know she has seen, and finding it may cost her "a day here, a name there."
  • Dez privately asked Magister Ilex Verbena what would stop him from killing Lady Cattelya. Verbena answered that others hold the same knowledge, and that Lord Vanda and the household would pay for it while Dez fled. Calanthe did not hear the exchange, and Dez backed down.
  • As Magister Ilex Verbena walked back to the Potentilla manor, several of the extra city-uniformed guards stepped out from among the orange trees and fell in behind her.
  • Althea summoned Calanthe Orchidaceae to Lord Vanda Potentilla's study and told her to bring only those she trusted with her life. Calanthe brought the whole party.
  • In his study, Lord Vanda Potentilla told Calanthe that every lesson cost Lady Cattelya memories: vacations, meetings, even household staff. In the first week they had been told her help was the Queen's will, and that the alternative was a garrison on the house. He said the Potentillas had been stewards of Cinquefoil since before the Empire, and that Althea was part of why they chose Calanthe (whom Calanthe told the party was adopted).
  • Lord Vanda Potentilla revealed that Magister Ilex Verbena was looking for a person in the Potentilla house. He would say no more, for fear the Magister could take the answer from Calanthe instead of from her mother. He said Lady Cattelya was holding on as long as she could to protect them all. The party weighed options (Dez using Alter Self to pose as Cattelya, Cleke sneaking into the workroom) and decided to hold their cards for now.
  • Late that night, after the party had returned from the Petal Rest festival, the door of the Potentilla workroom stood open with a lamp burning and the dust sheets pulled off. Lady Cattelya's draft memorial to The Grey Wilt filled all six tables: a square of hundreds of detailed portraits around one faceless woman standing above the crowd. Cattelya had rubbed that face out so many times the paper had worn through. On the newest draft, the face had been cut out with a knife and taken.

Log

Session 18

Canon 2 to 3 Caducan, Petal Rest in Cinquefoil: Cassimir unmasks at the water stairs, Lady Cattelya forgets Bryony, Aeneas blackmails Calanthe into the betrothal, Althea is revealed as Amorphia, and Aeneas accepts Sable's offer.

Session 18

Quick Reference

Session #: 18

Played On: 2026-10-11

In-world Date: 2 Caducan 301 Bloom to 3 Caducan 301 Bloom

PCs Present: Calanthe Orchidaceae, Kirill Amaterasu, Dez, Cleke (joined late)

Recap

The session opened in the late afternoon of the 2nd of Caducan, the second day of Petal Rest in Cinquefoil. As the party left Potentilla Manor in masks and costumes, a house guard told Calanthe Orchidaceae that two print sellers were asking for her at the Garden Gate. They were Charlotte and Charybdis. Charybdis read a report off her slate: the bait had worked, and a young man in a fox mask had watched the party the day before. She expected him to come looking again that night.

In the Great Square the drums were deafening and every face was masked. The fox-masked man brushed past and caught Kirill Amaterasu's eye, then nodded toward the water stairs. The party split up. Calanthe got swept into a line dance called "the turn," lost him, and followed him through a dark alley under a flickering lamp. On the way she spotted a halfling in a bear mask lurking near the water stairs. When Kirill Amaterasu and Dez reached the stairs, the man took off his mask. He was Cassimir Blackthorn. He asked the party to reconsider Sable's work and admitted he was ready to accept bloodshed. Then Spadae unmasked and signaled that they had to leave. Before going, Cassimir told them three things. Sable was hunting the same thing the crown was after in Cinquefoil. The Aether Golem was already full, so she meant to hijack the operation here as she had in Blackthorn. And they could reach him through the bread cellar at the square.

The party ate festival food. Kirill and Calanthe then danced on a stage for a combined 46 performance and pulled the whole crowd in. Kirill spun a fire baton he lit with burning hands. At a strange vendor's blanket stall, Dez and Cleke joked about stealing. The vendor panicked and fled, leaving his wares behind. Dez took two daggers and a spear. Cleke took a longbow and an emerald ring, and left IOU notes. Back at the manor, Calanthe gave candied oranges to Lord Vanda Potentilla and Althea.

At breakfast the next morning, Lady Cattelya Potentilla did not recognize Bryony, who has served her tea for years. She described The Queen's Commission as a mural of The Grey Wilt, then left without touching her food. Aeneas Solanaceae arrived and announced that a crier was booked to proclaim his betrothal to Calanthe that night. In private, he blackmailed her. He claimed he held what the Magister wanted from her mother: the identity of a person Cattelya had been protecting. He would keep it hidden only if Calanthe went along with the betrothal, and she agreed. Afterward, Althea confirmed what Calanthe had worked out: Althea is Amorphia, the god the crown is hunting. Meanwhile Kirill searched Aeneas's room and found a letter from "S." Dez and Cleke tailed Aeneas to a building full of Cinquefoil guards and heard Sable ask whether he had considered her offer. He said yes, and the session ended there.

Key Events

  • Charybdis and Charlotte met the party at the Garden Gate of Potentilla Manor and reported that the bait had worked: a young man in a fox mask had watched the party during Petal Rest the day before.
  • In the Great Square of Cinquefoil, a young man in a red paper fox mask with a white blaze brushed past the party. He made eye contact with Kirill Amaterasu and tipped his head toward the water stairs.
  • The party split in the Great Square. Calanthe Orchidaceae followed the fox-masked man, while Kirill Amaterasu and Dez went straight to the water stairs.
  • Calanthe Orchidaceae got caught up in a Cinquefoil line dance called "the turn" and lost the fox-masked man. She followed him through a dark alley and saw a halfling in a bear mask hiding near the water stairs.
  • At the water stairs, the fox-masked man unmasked as Cassimir Blackthorn. Kirill Amaterasu and Dez saw a paper or letter tucked inside his coat.
  • Cassimir Blackthorn said Sable had been in Cinquefoil for some time and he had only just arrived. He urged the party to reconsider joining Sable. When Dez pressed him, he hesitated, then said a few broken eggs were worth it.
  • Calanthe Orchidaceae argued to Cassimir Blackthorn that their ideals were similar but the party had to protect what was left. She moved to tell him that his father, Lord Corvus Blackthorn, was in Cinquefoil looking for him.
  • Spadae unmasked as the bear-masked halfling and signaled Cassimir Blackthorn to leave. Cassimir told the party that Sable was searching for the same thing the crown sought in Cinquefoil. He said The Aether Golem was already full, so Sable planned to hijack the local operation as she had in Blackthorn.
  • Before leaving, Cassimir Blackthorn told the party they could reach him through the bread cellar at the square in Cinquefoil.
  • After the meeting, Kirill Amaterasu said Cassimir Blackthorn deserved a second chance. Dez stayed bitter about the betrayal.
  • Calanthe Orchidaceae bought candied orange peel for the party in the Great Square. A perception check confirmed that no one was watching them in the square that night.
  • Kirill Amaterasu and Calanthe Orchidaceae danced on a festival stage during Petal Rest for a combined performance of 46 and pulled the crowd and Dez up with them. Kirill spun a cloth-wrapped pole he had lit with burning hands, and people wove flowers into the party's hair, Calfur's included.
  • A vendor in Cinquefoil sold wares off a blanket: daggers, a spear, a bow, dancing shoes, and a shepherding staff. When Dez and Cleke joked about stealing, he yanked his blanket over himself, yelled that guards were coming, and fled, leaving everything behind.
  • Dez took two new daggers and a spear from the abandoned stall. Cleke took a longbow and a ring set with a large emerald, and left IOU notes.
  • Back at Potentilla Manor after midnight, Calanthe Orchidaceae gave candied oranges to Lord Vanda Potentilla, who was working late, and to Althea. Lady Cattelya Potentilla had already retired.
  • At breakfast on the second terrace of Potentilla Manor, Lady Cattelya Potentilla asked Bryony, who had brought her tea every morning for years, whether she was new.
  • Lady Cattelya Potentilla told the party that The Queen's Commission was a mural of The Grey Wilt, a plague from the start of The Acanthine era. She left her food and tea untouched, and Lord Vanda Potentilla was visibly grieved.
  • Aeneas Solanaceae arrived at breakfast with flowers and account books. He said he forgave Calanthe Orchidaceae and that a crier was booked to announce their betrothal on the closing night of Petal Rest. Lord Vanda Potentilla whispered to Calanthe that he had never blessed any such thing.
  • Lord Vanda Potentilla explained the timeline to Calanthe Orchidaceae. After she vanished, Aeneas Solanaceae claimed to be her fiancé with some evidence. Vanda gave him work because he had nothing, and over about a month Aeneas took on more of the family business.
  • Calanthe Orchidaceae persuaded Lord Vanda Potentilla to take Ana (sp?) as his intern, then privately told Ana she was now Calanthe's spy.
  • In her office at Potentilla Manor, Calanthe Orchidaceae told Aeneas Solanaceae they had never been in a real relationship and had parted ways before she was kidnapped. Aeneas brought up their time at The Academy and her failed Florescence attempts.
  • Aeneas Solanaceae blackmailed Calanthe Orchidaceae. He claimed to possess what Magister Ilex Verbena wanted from Lady Cattelya Potentilla: the identity of a person Cattelya had refused to name for months. He would keep it hidden only if Calanthe willingly took part in the betrothal. She agreed.
  • Dez and Cleke overheard the blackmail from the second-floor balcony window of Potentilla Manor. Kirill Amaterasu, waiting by the door, did not.
  • After Aeneas Solanaceae left, Calanthe Orchidaceae said his family was from Blackthorn, upper class but deposed. Cleke raised the worry that Magister Ilex Verbena, who has her own office in Potentilla Manor, may already have reported the party.
  • Calanthe Orchidaceae rolled a natural 20 on persuasion to stop Kirill Amaterasu from killing Aeneas Solanaceae.
  • Pressed in private, Althea told Calanthe Orchidaceae that the crown sought a person whose identity only Lady Cattelya Potentilla, Lord Vanda Potentilla, and Althea knew.
  • Calanthe Orchidaceae realized that Althea is Amorphia, god of beauty, splendor, glory, and the arts, and that the crown is hunting her. Althea confirmed it with a nod. She warned that knowing made everyone less safe, and said Lord Vanda Potentilla had begged Calanthe not to come home so she would not see her mother this way.
  • Kirill Amaterasu searched Aeneas Solanaceae's room and found two letters. One was sealed, unscented, and addressed to Magister Ilex Verbena. The other, already opened, was signed "S" and promised Aeneas rewards for information about the Magister's business at Potentilla Manor.
  • Dez and Cleke flew above the clouds to tail Aeneas Solanaceae out of Potentilla Manor to a small, nondescript building in Cinquefoil. Inside he met many people in Cinquefoilian armor.
  • Inside the building, Dez and Cleke heard Sable ask Aeneas Solanaceae whether he had considered her offer, and Aeneas answered, "Yes, I have."
