Log

  • 2 Caducan 301 Bloom: Late afternoon on the second day of Petal Rest, Charybdis and Charlotte report at the Garden Gate of Potentilla Manor that the bait worked (Charybdis and Charlotte Report at the Garden Gate). In the Great Square of Cinquefoil a fox-masked man leads the party to the Cinquefoil Water Stairs and unmasks as Cassimir Blackthorn; he leaves with Spadae after warning that Sable means to hijack the Crown's operation here and naming The Bread Cellar as a contact point (Cassimir Unmasks at the Water Stairs). Kirill and Calanthe dance on a festival stage (Festival Dance in the Great Square), and a blanket-stall vendor flees from Dez and Cleke (The Blanket Vendor Flees). After midnight Calanthe gives candied oranges to Lord Vanda Potentilla and Althea.
  • 3 Caducan 301 Bloom: At breakfast Lady Cattelya Potentilla does not recognize Bryony (Lady Cattelya Forgets Bryony) and Aeneas Solanaceae announces the betrothal (Aeneas Announces the Betrothal at Breakfast). Calanthe places Ana as Vanda's intern and her own spy. In private Aeneas blackmails Calanthe with the hidden person's identity and she agrees (Aeneas Blackmails Calanthe). Althea confirms she is Amorphia (Althea Revealed as Amorphia). Kirill finds two letters in Aeneas's room (Kirill Searches Aeneas's Room), and Dez and Cleke tail Aeneas to Sable's Meeting House in Cinquefoil, where he accepts Sable's offer (Aeneas Accepts Sable's Offer).

Session 19

Canon Final day of Petal Rest in Cinquefoil: the party tails Aeneas to Sable, recovers the memorial cutout, hears Althea's confession, spares Aeneas and calls his bluff, Calanthe performs the betrothal announcement, and green fire falls on the burning city.

Session 19

Quick Reference

Session #: 19

Played On: 2026-10-25

In-world Date: 3 Caducan 301 Bloom

PCs Present: Dez, Cleke, Val, Vesperae Locke, Kirill Amaterasu, Calanthe Orchidaceae

Recap

On the final day of Petal Rest in Cinquefoil, the party split up to deal with Aeneas Solanaceae, who is blackmailing Calanthe Orchidaceae with the secret that Althea is the city's hidden god. Cleke, Dez, Val and Vesperae Locke tailed him to Sable's Meeting House in Cinquefoil, where Sable offered him "the highest seat in the nation" and a small chest of gold. He promised an answer that night, after the announcement (Sable Offers Aeneas the Highest Seat). Vesperae warned Dez by Sending of two shadowy figures on the rooftops, and Cleke trapped them in a Web (Cleke Webs the Rooftop Pursuers). At the Abandoned Printing Press, Aeneas mocked Dez and threatened his mother in what used to be Loquat. Dez found a hidden floor compartment anyway, its explosive trap inert, and took The Face Cut from the Memorial Draft. Dez and Cleke ensnared Aeneas, gagged him and dragged him back to the others (Dez Confronts Aeneas at the Printing Press).

At Potentilla Manor, Ana walked in on Kirill Amaterasu and Kefra Chico as they searched Aeneas's office, holding the Letter from S to Aeneas and the Sealed Letter to Magister Verbena. Kirill's lie fell flat, and Val greeted him with a punch that threw him about 20 feet (Ana Walks In on the Office Search). In her childhood room, Althea confirmed she is Cinquefoil's god of culture and arts. She admitted that she and Calanthe's parents blocked Calanthe's Florescence to keep her safe, and begged her to save the family (Althea Confesses Her Part in Calanthe's Florescence).

Regrouped, the party read Sable's letter. Dez and Kirill wanted Aeneas dead and dumped in the river, but Calanthe refused and tended his wounds. Remembering that the last deal with Sable cost them The Aether Golem and Spadae, they chose cunning over siding with Sable or the Queen's Thorns. They sent word to Elara Vane, Dez spent 4 story points on a letter to Sirocco, and Calanthe wrote Calanthe's Letter to Cassimir to Cassimir Blackthorn (The Party Spares Aeneas). Calanthe also ritual-cast Identify on the items Dez and Cleke took with an IOU last session. Her Insight showed that Aeneas's talk of copies was a bluff, and Dez traced a forgery of the cutout for her to hold (Calanthe Calls Aeneas's Bluff).

That evening Calanthe walked out on Aeneas's arm before reporters, Lord Vanda Potentilla, Lady Cattelya Potentilla and Grand Magister Magister Ilex Verbena, and gave the best performance of her life (The Betrothal Announced at Potentilla Manor, Aeneas's Betrothal Bargain). Cleke and Kefra took The Uncut Memorial Mural from Cattelya's workshop but found nothing there that could restore her memories (Cleke and Kefra Take the Uncut Mural, Restore Lady Cattelya's Memories). Vesperae spotted guests in steel-toed boots she took for Sable's people, and a familiar tiefling woman hid something in the bushes for Dez. From the manor roof, Val saw dozens of sickly neon-green fireballs falling toward the manor while green, blue and red fires already burned across the city (Green Fire Falls on Cinquefoil).

Key Events

  • On the final day of Petal Rest, Cleke, Dez, Val and Vesperae Locke tailed Aeneas Solanaceae from Potentilla Manor to a warehouse (Sable's Meeting House in Cinquefoil) in a canal-side market district of Cinquefoil. Dez and Cleke flew to the roof skylights, and Val and Vesperae listened from an alley window with a "secret romantic rendezvous" cover story.
  • Inside the warehouse, Sable sat with four soldiers in Cinquefoilian armor and two cloaked agents. She offered Aeneas Solanaceae "the highest seat in the nation" once she succeeds, and gave him a small chest of gold as a sign of good faith. Aeneas said he would give his answer tonight, after the announcement.
  • Val left the warehouse district for Potentilla Manor. Vesperae Locke stayed in the market district, posing as a noblewoman on holiday.
  • Vesperae Locke noticed two shadowy figures, dark even in the near-noon sun, following Dez and Cleke across the rooftops of Cinquefoil. She used Sending to warn Dez: "Don't make it obvious, but you are being tailed."
  • Cleke led Dez through low turns down the alleys of Cinquefoil and cast Web across the rooftops over a narrow alley. Behind them, the pursuers' frustrated voices showed they were stuck in it. Cleke lost Aeneas Solanaceae in the crowds, but Dez rerolled with Lucky and spotted him again.
  • Aeneas Solanaceae wandered for a while, then entered an abandoned printing press in the old scholarly printing district of southeastern Cinquefoil, which emptied after Loquat was founded. Dez and Cleke got in through a rotted back door and watched him pocket something from a desk.
  • Dez confronted Aeneas Solanaceae in the printing press while Cleke stayed hidden with a bow and Ensnaring Strike ready. Aeneas claimed he was doing survey work for House Potentilla and produced a handful of papers. He mocked Dez as a parentless slum rat from Loquat, called himself a graduate of The Academy, and said the Magister was expecting him.
  • When Dez said he would kill him, Aeneas Solanaceae answered that a killing in the city had no justification and would get the whole party thrown out. He then threatened Dez's mother in the tenements of what used to be Loquat, along with her friend, the innkeeper she meets with and her landlord, until she was alone and last. He also told Dez that Calanthe Orchidaceae was free to keep a lover on the side and that he had no interest in her.
  • At the printing press, Aeneas Solanaceae turned out his pockets, which held nothing but the papers. Dez passed a DC 20 Insight check, caught Aeneas glancing at a bare patch of floor and found a hollow square there: a hidden compartment. Aeneas put on a nervous sweat.
  • Dez spent 4 story points (12 down to 8) to auto-crit an Investigation check on the hidden compartment in the printing press. It was a push-click drawer with an explosive trap wired to go off if it was opened wrong, but the trap was badly installed and inert. Aeneas Solanaceae's earlier panic had been an act. Now he showed real confusion, fear and anger. Dez took the cutout from the compartment (The Face Cut from the Memorial Draft).
  • In Aeneas's office at Potentilla Manor, Kirill Amaterasu and Kefra Chico already held the Letter from S to Aeneas and the Sealed Letter to Magister Verbena when Ana walked in. She was carrying ledgers from her work with Lord Vanda Potentilla. The GM stated that Ana is on the party's side.
  • In Aeneas's office, Kirill Amaterasu told Ana that Calanthe had sent him to fetch something for Aeneas. Kefra Chico helped, and Kirill chose to fail the DC 10 Deception check. Confused and scared, Ana searched her notebook for a "secret code" she might have forgotten and asked if she had done something wrong.
  • Val walked into Aeneas's office, saw Kirill Amaterasu holding the letters and a frightened Ana, and punched Kirill in the chest for 4 damage, throwing him about 20 feet. Ana tried to bandage Kirill's head with old paper. Val stabilized him with a healer's kit and a cotton swab taped to his forehead.
  • Val, Kirill Amaterasu and Kefra Chico traded what they had learned (the warehouse meeting and the two letters), put Aeneas's office back the way they found it, and went to collect Calanthe Orchidaceae.
  • In her room at Potentilla Manor, surrounded by childhood keepsakes, Calanthe Orchidaceae sat with Althea, who had just confirmed that she is the god of culture and arts in Cinquefoil. Afraid for the first time Calanthe had ever seen, Althea said the family did not know what to do and that Calanthe was their only hope.
  • Althea told Calanthe Orchidaceae that she and Calanthe's parents shared the blame for Calanthe's failed Florescence. Calanthe's talent had drawn too much attention at a time when people were already watching the family, for the same reason Lady Cattelya Potentilla is sick now, and they got in her way to keep her safe. Althea begged Calanthe to save the family and her, and Calanthe promised to do her best.
  • Val, Ana, Kirill Amaterasu and Kefra Chico burst into Calanthe's room. Calanthe Orchidaceae kicked her keepsakes under the bed, and Althea left with an offer to help if anyone needed it.
  • The group picked up Vesperae Locke in the market district of Cinquefoil and met Dez and Cleke. Cleke had wrapped Aeneas Solanaceae's legs in thorns with Ensnaring Strike, and the pair had disarmed and gagged him and dragged him there. Aeneas now limps.
  • Kirill Amaterasu handed Calanthe Orchidaceae the open Letter from S to Aeneas, in which Sable offered riches and power in exchange for spying on House Potentilla. Dez and Kirill pushed to kill Aeneas Solanaceae and dump him in the river. Calanthe refused and tended his wounds.
  • The party weighed siding with Sable or the Queen's Thorns. They recalled that the last deal with Sable cost them The Aether Golem and Spadae, and decided to play it cunning rather than aggressive. Dez checked the warehouse and found Sable gone.
  • The party sent a message to Elara Vane, their main contact, by the method she had given them. Dez paid 4 story points (8 down to 4) to also send a letter to Sirocco.
  • Calanthe Orchidaceae spent an hour ritual-casting Identify on the items Dez and Cleke had taken with an IOU last session: Dez's two daggers and spear, and Cleke's bow and ring.
  • Calanthe Orchidaceae wrote a poem-like letter to Cassimir Blackthorn (Persuasion 22). It told him that people make mistakes and that he should talk to his father, but that he has other family, Calanthe included, and that she needs him to come and help her.
  • The party planned for the evening announcements at Potentilla Manor, covering both Aeneas and Calanthe's engagement and Lady Cattelya Potentilla's mural. Calanthe Orchidaceae and Kirill Amaterasu would handle Aeneas Solanaceae, and Cleke and Kefra Chico would sneak into the workshop to destroy the new painting and find a way to restore Cattelya's memories. Val, Vesperae Locke and Dez would keep watch.
  • Calanthe Orchidaceae ungagged Aeneas Solanaceae and played the nervous wreck. Aeneas said she was responsible for her friends, claimed he had copies and a letter ready to send, and demanded that Dez return what he stole on his knees. Calanthe's Insight showed it was a bluff: his only letter was the one Kirill Amaterasu had found, and he had no leverage at all.
  • Calanthe Orchidaceae sent Kirill Amaterasu to fetch "the thing Dez stole" and used Sending to tell Dez to make a duplicate. In Calanthe's workshop, Dez traced the cutout onto canvas with a dagger (roll of 19), kept the real one and gave Kirill the forgery. Calanthe insisted on holding it herself and staying at Aeneas Solanaceae's side.
  • Calanthe Orchidaceae and Aeneas Solanaceae walked out to the flower-decked front of Potentilla Manor, where reporters waited with Lord Vanda Potentilla, Lady Cattelya Potentilla and Grand Magister Magister Ilex Verbena. Calanthe played a woman in love more convincingly than she ever had.
  • Cleke and Kefra Chico searched Lady Cattelya Potentilla's workshop, and Kefra found a new version of the mural, rolled up and uncut, which they took. Nothing in the room could restore Cattelya's memory, so they concluded the means must be on Magister Ilex Verbena's person.
  • Vesperae Locke, blending into the announcement crowd at Potentilla Manor, saw more guards than usual. She also saw several reporters and well-dressed guests wearing thick, steel-toed boots, which she took to mean Sable's people were present.
  • Dez, perched on a stable roof near Potentilla Manor with a ready spell and 120 feet of range, saw a familiar tiefling woman hide something in the bushes for him to collect. She hid it where only he could see.
  • Val, on the roof of Potentilla Manor, felt the air shake and his adrenaline surge. He looked up and saw dozens of large balls of sickly neon-green fire falling toward the manor, while green, blue and red fires already burned across Cinquefoil. The session ended there.

Log

Petal Rest

Developing Cinquefoil's multi-day festival, ending with the Potentilla betrothal announcement on its final night.

Petal Rest

Quick Reference

Kind: holiday

Date / Range: 1 Caducan

Description

Petal Rest falls on 1 Caducan each year, the first day of the tenth month, when the year's flowering is held to be over. Most of Elarosea keeps it quietly: petals swept from the doorstep, a meal, a night without work. Cinquefoil keeps it loud. The city spends the day hanging every wall with paper flowers, red, yellow and green, and the night in a parade of dancers, drum lines and feathered headdresses that runs from the gate of the forty dancers to the great square, with fireworks over the lower town at the end. The masks come out at dusk. On the eve, every household bakes a name-bread, the names of its dead pressed into the crust, and breaks it on the night without speaking them (see Cinquefoil Cuisine). Ateliers in the artists' quarter work for weeks on the headdresses, and the season's cut for Caelrosa is first seen on Petal Rest night. Petal Rest opens on the first of Caducan, marking the close of the flowering season. The day fills Cinquefoil with carnival stalls, archery games, fountain jousting, and side-bet prediction markets. At sundown the whole square puts on festival masks, a tradition that gives the crowds anonymity through the evening. Petal Rest is a festival held in Cinquefoil, beginning on 1 Caducan. Everyone goes masked and in costume. In the Great Square of Cinquefoil the drums are deafening, food stalls sell festival fare such as candied orange peel, and performers dance on open stages. Revelers weave flowers into the hair of dancers and onlookers alike. Crowds join a Cinquefoil line dance called "the turn", which can sweep a person away from their companions. On the festival's closing night a crier can be booked to make public proclamations, such as a betrothal.

  • 2 Caducan 301 Bloom: On the second day, Kirill Amaterasu and Calanthe Orchidaceae danced on a festival stage and drew the crowd in; flowers were woven into the party's hair, Calfur's included (Festival Dance in the Great Square, Session 18).
  • 3 Caducan 301 Bloom: Aeneas Solanaceae announced he had booked a crier to proclaim his betrothal to Calanthe on the closing night (Aeneas's Betrothal Bargain).

Petal Rest is a multi-day festival in Cinquefoil that opens on 1 Caducan. In 301 Bloom its final day fell on 3 Caducan. Visitors could pass as nobles on holiday in the canal-side markets. That closing night, reporters and guests gathered at the flower-decked front of Potentilla Manor to hear the betrothal of Calanthe Orchidaceae and Aeneas Solanaceae announced (The Betrothal Announced at Potentilla Manor). The same evening, green fire fell on the city (Green Fire Falls on Cinquefoil, Session 19).

Significance

  • The Cinquefoil observance was proposed during Session 16 prep on 2026-09-26 and is pending the GM's ruling.
  • Session 16 of The Queen's Commission opens on Petal Rest, 1 Caducan 301 Bloom. The party enters the city on the day of the parade.

Log

The Calendar

Developing The in-world calendar: twelve botanical months, four seasons, four festivals, years counted in Bloom

00

The Calendar

Quick Reference

Kind: calendar

Date / Range: 346-day year, twelve months, reckoned in Bloom

Description

A year in Elarosea runs twelve months and totals 346 days. The months trace the life of a flower, from the first green of the year through full bloom to the fall of petals and the bare turn before the next Bloom:

1. Vesthen (30 days) 2. Quirren (28 days) 3. Solvance (30 days) 4. Folial (28 days) 5. Verdane (30 days) 6. Caulix (28 days) 7. Prelin (28 days) 8. Roseil (32 days) 9. Pollant (30 days) 10. Caducan (28 days) 11. Achenne (28 days) 12. Rothen (26 days)

Years are reckoned in Bloom, written after the year: for example 12 Pollant 301 Bloom (see The Bloom). Year 0 Bloom is the start of Queen Acantha Rosaceae's reign, when the calendar reset; Year 1 Bloom is the Consumption of Veritas. Recorded eras, oldest first: The Unrecorded, then The Acanthine, in which the Bloom count runs. The current in-world date is 12 Pollant 301 Bloom.

The seasons

Elarosea keeps four seasons of three months each, following the same botanical arc as the month names. They belong to the calendar and the land, not to the Rose Throne, which by the queen's making flowers without rest (see The Bloom).

  • Quickening (Vesthen, Quirren, Solvance) — the first green of the year. Seeds wake, the days lengthen, and the New Year is sworn at Verdant Oaths.
  • Verdure (Folial, Verdane, Caulix) — full leaf and stem. Growth runs ahead of flower and the world is at its greenest.
  • Bloomtide (Prelin, Roseil, Pollant) — the flowering peak. Roses open in Roseil, the longest month, and the season holds both Roseil Night and Starsfall.
  • Petalfall (Caducan, Achenne, Rothen) — petals drop, seed sets, and the year wanes. It opens with Petal Rest and ends in the short, bare month of Rothen before the next Quickening.

The week

The week is six days, named for the life of a single bloom. The count begins anew with the first of each month:

1. Seedday (Sd) 2. Rootday (Rd) 3. Leafday (Lf) 4. Budday (Bd) 5. Bloomday (Bm) 6. Fallday (Fd)

Because every month opens on Seedday, a date's weekday is fixed year to year: the first of any month is always a Seedday, so both Verdant Oaths (1 Vesthen) and Petal Rest (1 Caducan) fall on one. A thirty-day month is exactly five weeks; the shorter months leave a few remnant days before the next month restarts the count. Dates are written without the weekday — day, month, year Bloom — and the named days mark market, rest, and labour rather than the dating of events.

Festivals

Four fixed festivals anchor the year, each on the same date every Bloom (see The Bloom):

  • [[Verdant Oaths]] — 1 Vesthen, the first day of the year. Oaths are sworn for the year to come; in 302 Bloom the Crown set it as the day the Everbloom Reform takes force.
  • [[Roseil Night]] — 16 Roseil, at the height of the rose month.
  • [[Starsfall]] — 7 Pollant, kept in Benz as the Festival of Shooting Stars. On 7 Pollant 301 Bloom a true star fell on Benz (Crash at Benz).
  • [[Petal Rest]] — 1 Caducan, as the petals begin to fall.

Significance

The calendar is the queen's. Its year resets to one at her accession and counts her reign in Blooms, one for each time the Rose Throne comes into flower (see The Bloom). The seasons carry a quiet irony: the land greens, blooms, and sheds its petals in Petalfall every year, yet the throne at its centre never rests, flowering even as the rest of Elarosea dies back. The festivals cut ordinary time into a shape everyone keeps, from the oaths of Verdant Oaths to the falling petals of Petal Rest. Because the records that would fix Year 0 belonged to Veritas, whom the queen consumed in Year 1, the calendar's own beginning rests on her word alone.

The Crown's operation in Cinquefoil targets Amorphia for god-consumption, the same mechanism the Aether Golem used against Valerius at Blackthorn. Veritas

Developing God of Truth; consumed by Queen Acantha beneath Caelrosa; his ledger resurrected Elara Aurum and left her immortal, and his residual presence in Blackthorn suppressed the divine being incubating inside Calfur.

Veritas

Quick Reference

Domains: truth, records

Divine Status: consumed

Alignment:

Holy Symbol:

Worshippers:

Portfolio

Domains, spheres of influence, and symbols. God of Truths and Records. Consumed by Queen Acantha Rosaceae in Year 1 of the current calendar. The act granted Acantha her immortality, though how consuming a deity produced that result remains unknown. Veritas's locus is The Index, the repository of the oldest and truest records, the one place his power concentrates into a single incarnate presence. He maintains a Ledger that records only what is true; writing a falsehood in it contradicts the definition of his being, and when he is weakened that certainty weakens with him. He is not a god of combat, which made him genuinely vulnerable when Acantha forced a direct fight.

Worship & Clergy

  • 24 Pollant 301 Bloom: Nal'Szomn confirmed The Index as Veritas's home and the site where Queen Acantha Rosaceae first sealed a god into herself. See Party Visits the Dreamcatcher.

Myths

Stories told about them, true or otherwise. Before Acantha consumed him, she came to him at the lowest point of her life and asked for one thing: the truth of whether Elara Aurum had survived the Grey Wilt. He kept it as he kept all others, written and shut away, offered to no one. His refusal to tell her a single true thing she needed was the last reason she took his power by force.

Connections

  • Queen Acantha Rosaceae
  • Session 15

Log

Description

Veritas is the God of Truth. Centuries before 301 Bloom, Queen Acantha Rosaceae descended beneath Caelrosa, defeated Veritas, and consumed the god's divine power (see Consumption of Veritas). Acantha then used the ledger Veritas carried to write Elara Vane back to life after she died of The Grey Wilt, leaving her immortal (see Resurrection of Elara Aurum). The party later stole that same ledger in the Grand Archives Heist. Though the god was consumed long ago, a residual divine presence lingered in Blackthorn. While it persisted there, it suppressed the being incubating inside Calfur. When the party left the city on 25 Pollant 301, that check ended. God of Truth. Queen Acantha Rosaceae defeated and consumed him beneath Caelrosa after the gods ignored her people during The Grey Wilt. His ledger could rewrite life and death; Acantha used it to resurrect Elara Vane and leave her immortal centuries ago. The party later stole the same ledger in the Grand Archives Heist (see Veritas's Ledger). After his consumption a residual divine presence lingered in Blackthorn, suppressing the growth of the divine being inside Calfur; that suppression ended when the party left the city.

Aeneas Announces the Betrothal at Breakfast

Aeneas Blackmails Calanthe

Developing In Calanthe's office Aeneas claims to hold the identity Magister Verbena wants from Lady Cattelya and will keep it hidden only if Calanthe goes along with the betrothal; she agrees, Dez and Cleke overhear from the balcony, and a natural 20 persuasion stops Kirill from killing him.

Aeneas Blackmails Calanthe

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 3 Caducan 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Potentilla Manor

Participants: Aeneas Solanaceae, Calanthe Orchidaceae, Dez, Cleke, Kirill Amaterasu

Session: Session 18

What Happened

In her office at Potentilla Manor on 3 Caducan 301 Bloom, Calanthe Orchidaceae told Aeneas Solanaceae they had never been in a real relationship and had parted ways before she was kidnapped. Aeneas brought up their time at The Academy and her failed Florescence attempts.

Then he named his price. He claimed to hold what Magister Ilex Verbena wanted from Lady Cattelya Potentilla: the identity of the person Cattelya had refused to name for months. He would keep it hidden only if Calanthe willingly took part in the betrothal. She agreed (see Aeneas's Betrothal Bargain).

Dez and Cleke overheard everything from the second-floor balcony window. Kirill Amaterasu, waiting by the door, did not. After Aeneas left, Calanthe said his family was from Blackthorn, upper class but deposed. Cleke worried that Verbena, who keeps her own office in the manor, may already have reported the party. Kirill wanted to kill Aeneas, and Calanthe stopped him with a natural 20 on persuasion.

Aether Golem Consumes Valerius

Canon Sable threw the Aether Core to Spadae at the training grounds; Spadae slotted it in and the Golem consumed the war god Valerius, ending the battle and completing Sable's plan.

Aether Golem Consumes Valerius

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 25 Pollant 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Blackthorn Training Grounds

Participants: Sable, Spadae, Valerius, The Aether Golem, Calanthe Orchidaceae, Kirill Amaterasu, Dez, Cassimir Blackthorn

Session: Session 14

What Happened

As Sable's guards began hauling the Golem body, Valerius walked in through the north gate of the Blackthorn Training Grounds and seized the guards' minds. Spadae climbed inside The Aether Golem and drove it by raw muscle. Dez, arriving late, tore a guard apart with Vampiric Touch. Calanthe was dropped mid-fight and revived with a potion. On the final round Sable fed The Aether Core to Spadae. The Golem lit up and Valerius was pulled inside, caged within the construct. Valerius arrived at Blackthorn Training Grounds, mind-controlled Sable's guards, and frightened the party. Sable ran to power The Aether Golem while the party stalled. Spadae climbed inside the Golem and piloted it, casting fireball from within. Cassimir Blackthorn was knocked unconscious. Calanthe Orchidaceae dropped to death saves before Dez revived her. On the final round Sable threw The Aether Core to Spadae, who slotted it in; the Golem activated and consumed Valerius, freeing the surviving guards.

  • Spadae piloted the construct and slotted in the Core after Sable threw it.

Althea Confesses Her Part in Calanthe's Florescence

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 3 Caducan 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Potentilla Manor

What Happened

Althea admitted that she and Calanthe's parents shared the blame for Calanthe's failed Florescence. She begged Calanthe to save the family and her. Calanthe promised to do her best.

Then Val, Ana, Kirill Amaterasu and Kefra Chico burst into the room. Calanthe kicked her keepsakes under the bed, and Althea left with an offer to help if anyone needed it.

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Loquat

Participants: Magister Corbin Locke, Vesperae Locke

What Happened

A great beast attacked Loquat, killing many inhabitants and leaving the city decimated. Cassimir Unmasks at the Water Stairs

Developing The fox-masked man leads the party to the Cinquefoil water stairs and unmasks as Cassimir; he urges them to reconsider Sable, accepts bloodshed, then leaves with Spadae after warning that Sable means to hijack the Crown's Cinquefoil operation and naming the bread cellar as a contact point.

Cassimir Unmasks at the Water Stairs

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 2 Caducan 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Cinquefoil Water Stairs

Participants: Cassimir Blackthorn, Spadae, Kirill Amaterasu, Dez, Calanthe Orchidaceae

Session: Session 18

What Happened

On the evening of 2 Caducan 301 Bloom the drums in the Great Square of Cinquefoil were deafening and every face was masked. A young man in a red paper fox mask with a white blaze brushed past the party, caught Kirill Amaterasu's eye, and tipped his head toward the water stairs. The party split. Kirill and Dez went straight to the Cinquefoil Water Stairs. Calanthe Orchidaceae tried to follow the man, got swept into the line dance called "the turn", lost him, and then followed him through a dark alley under a flickering lamp. On the way she spotted a halfling in a bear mask hiding near the stairs.

At the stairs the man took off his mask. He was Cassimir Blackthorn. Kirill and Dez saw a paper or letter tucked inside his coat. Cassimir said Sable had been in Cinquefoil for some time and he had only just arrived. He urged the party to reconsider joining Sable. When Dez pressed him, he hesitated, then said a few broken eggs were worth it. Calanthe argued that their ideals were similar but the party had to protect what was left, and moved to tell him that his father, Lord Corvus Blackthorn, was in the city looking for him.

The bear-masked halfling unmasked as Spadae and signaled that they had to leave. Before going, Cassimir told the party that Sable was hunting the same thing the Crown sought in Cinquefoil, and that The Aether Golem was already full, so she meant to hijack the operation here as she had in Blackthorn. He said they could reach him through The Bread Cellar at the square.

Afterward Kirill said Cassimir deserved a second chance. Dez stayed bitter about the betrayal.

Cassimir Visits the Safe House

Canon Cassimir Blackthorn arrives at the Hive safe house using the secret knock Sable taught him, shows Theron's Letter, and asks the party to meet him at the training grounds at noon.

Cassimir Visits the Safe House

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 24 Pollant 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Blackthorn

Participants: Cassimir Blackthorn, Calanthe Orchidaceae, Val, Edmund Vane, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Vesperae Locke, Spadae

Session: Session 13

What Happened

Late on 24 Pollant 301, Cassimir Blackthorn knocked at Edmund Vane's door using the Hive entry code Sable had taught him. Val and Calanthe Orchidaceae met him in the rain. He produced Theron's Letter, a never-sent letter in Theron Blackthorn's hand, as evidence Sable had personally known his brother. He begged for the party's help, invited them to the training grounds at noon the following day, warned them to be suspicious of The Hive, then ran off into the dark. Details of this visit are not described in the available session notes. Cassimir Blackthorn was present at the training grounds when Sable made her pitch to the party later on 25 Pollant 301, suggesting he had already made contact and followed the party there. A three-then-one knock at the safe house door used the Hive's secret code. Edmund Vane identified Cassimir Blackthorn through the spyhole and warned the party that only Sable could have taught the boy the knock, making the visit a possible decoy. The party split: Dez and Kirill Amaterasu slipped out the back trap door toward The Aether Golem, while Calanthe Orchidaceae and Val opened the door. Vesperae Locke and Spadae stayed with the prisoners. Cassimir begged the party to help bring Theron Blackthorn back and showed them Theron's Letter, a never-sent letter in Theron's own hand that Sable had given him as proof she knew his brother. He admitted he did not know Sable's full plan. He said she had told him where to find the party and taught him the knock, and he asked them to come to Blackthorn's training grounds at noon. He warned that the Hive 'is not all it turns out to be' before hugging them and running off. The party is split: Dez and Kirill Amaterasu move independently toward The Aether Golem, while the others hold a noon appointment at the training grounds through Cassimir Blackthorn's invitation. Cassimir's warning about The Hive plants doubt about their own handlers' agenda.

Log

Consumption of Veritas

Developing Queen Acantha descended beneath Caelrosa, defeated and consumed Veritas the God of Truth, then waged war on every god that failed her people during the Grey Wilt.

Consumption of Veritas

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 1 Bloom

Era: Year 0 Reckoning

Location:

Participants: Queen Acantha Rosaceae, Veritas

Session:

What Happened

In Year 1 of the current calendar, Queen Acantha Rosaceae consumed Veritas, the god of Truths and Records. The act granted her immortality. The calendar had been reset to Year 0 when she began her reign; Year 1 marked the year of the consumption. The exact location and the mechanism by which divinity-consumption confers immortality are unknown.

  • Queen Acantha Rosaceae became immortal.
  • Veritas ceased to exist as an active deity.
  • The calendar was fixed with Year 0 as the start of Acantha's reign, making this the founding event of the current era (campaign year: 301).
  • Elara Vane (then Elara Aurum), surviving against all expectation, dedicated the next 300 years to finding a way to reverse the consumption.

The consumption required locating The Index, the locus of Veritas's domain, where he could be engaged as a single incarnate thing rather than a diffuse presence. The battle was long and arduous. Veritas is the god of knowledge, not combat, and Acantha ultimately won by luck as much as preparation.

She arrived with an artifact capable of absorbing divine essence, but needed Veritas weakened before she could use it. She knew that his Ledger records only what is true and that this property is tied to his own coherence: weaken him and the certainty of that truth weakens with him. While he faltered, she wrote a new entry in his Ledger: that Elara Aurum had survived The Grey Wilt. She believed this to be false. The contradiction, a lie forced into the instrument of absolute truth, shattered Veritas's coherence enough for Acantha to absorb his essence through the prepared artifact.

Consequences

Connections

  • Queen Acantha Rosaceae
  • Veritas
  • Elara Vane
  • Session 15

Log

Description

When The Grey Wilt killed thousands and the gods withheld their aid, Queen Acantha Rosaceae descended beneath Caelrosa and defeated Veritas, the God of Truth, consuming his divine power. She then waged war on the gods that had failed her people. The stolen divinity eventually drove her mad. Veritas's ledger, which Acantha retained after the consumption, was later used to resurrect Elara Vane (Resurrection of Elara Aurum) and was eventually stolen by the party in the Grand Archives Heist. After the gods ignored her people throughout The Grey Wilt, Queen Acantha Rosaceae descended beneath Caelrosa and defeated Veritas, the God of Truth, consuming his divine power. She then waged war on every god that had failed her people. The stolen power overwhelmed her sanity over time, eventually driving her into the seclusion she entered roughly five years before 301 Bloom.

Death of Roots Arcanist Quill

Established Roots Arcanist Quill is killed in the vault of Crown Works Installation Seven by the party after a close fight.

Death of Roots Arcanist Quill

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 24 Pollant 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Crown Works Installation Seven

Participants: Roots Arcanist Quill, Dez, Spadae, Val, Calanthe Orchidaceae, Cleke

Session: Session 12

What Happened

Roots Arcanist Quill stepped from the quiet of the sub-floor laboratory inside Crown Works Installation Seven while Sable's men pried the Aether Core from its pylons. He logged the party's treason and called the guards. His Evard's Black Tentacles held the party briefly; once free, they focused entirely on Quill. Calanthe Orchidaceae slowed him. Vesperae Locke applied Sacred Flame and Guiding Bolt. Spadae grappled him and threw him onto a live lightning conduit. Cleke struck with two arrows and a swarm. Quill landed a Vitriolic Spear on Val for 24 acid before Dez killed him with a Chromatic Orb through the head. Quill stepped from the shadows in the underground lab, declared this his ninth entry, charged the group with treason, and summoned three hidden guards. Maret opened with a crossbow bolt; Vesperae followed with Moonbeam. Quill replied with Evard's Black Tentacles (restraining Calanthe) then a Fireball dropping Calanthe to 11 hp. His death has not been confirmed in the surviving record of this session. Spadae hook-shot to a lightning conduit, grappled Quill, and threw him onto it for 18 lightning damage, leaving him impaled and shocked. Calanthe landed a Vicious Mockery and withdrew toward the exit. Cleke put two arrows and a swarm into the arcanist, leaving him barely standing. Quill cast Vitriolic Spear before he fell: Val took 24 acid damage with 5d4 ongoing, while Spadae and Dez each took 12. Dez finished him with a first-level Chromatic Orb, driving an electric bolt through the impaled arcanist's head.

Destruction of Havari

Established The ancient beast escaped the sealed cave beneath Havari and destroyed the village before moving on to Loquat; discovered by the party on 16 Pollant 301

Destruction of Havari

Quick Reference

Dez and Kirill Captured by Sable's Agents

Developing Dez and Kirill tail the Scarred Watcher from the party's safe house to a building in Blackthorn, where crossbow agents shoot them down and Sable's operatives take them captive; they escape the following morning.

Dez and Kirill Captured by Sable's Agents

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 24 Pollant 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Blackthorn

Participants: Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, The Scarred Watcher, Sable

Session: Session 13

What Happened

While the rest of the party spoke with Cassimir Blackthorn inside, Dez and Kirill Amaterasu slipped out the back to watch over the The Aether Golem at the training grounds. They spotted The Scarred Watcher on a nearby rooftop, observing Cassimir as he left. They followed the figure across Blackthorn, were brought down by crossbow bolts mid-pursuit, and were hauled off by agents working for Sable. During or immediately after the events at Crown Works Installation Seven on 24 Pollant 301, agents working for Sable took Dez and Kirill Amaterasu and confined them in a stone cellar somewhere in Blackthorn. The two freed themselves before dawn: Kirill used his otters and Dez shaped the earth of the cell. They rejoined the rest of the party by morning on 25 Pollant 301. Dez and Kirill Amaterasu spotted The Scarred Watcher on a rooftop near the safe house. Dez flew across Blackthorn carrying Kirill, trailing the man to a building in another district, where four crossbow bolts tore them from the air. They fell unconscious and Sable's agents carried them off to a stone cellar. The pair woke bound and blindfolded. By mid-morning of 25 Pollant 301 Bloom, Kirill's summoned otters had freed their manacles and Dez shaped the stone ceiling open. They climbed out into a foundry district in Blackthorn and returned to the safe house, where Vesperae Locke's Prayer of Healing restored them.

Dez Awakens Flight

Developing Dez slips from the ceiling beams over the central chasm at Crown Works and instinctively awakens the power of flight as he falls

Dez Awakens Flight

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 24 Pollant 301

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Crown Works Installation Seven

Participants: Dez

Session: Session 12

What Happened

Attempting to cross the central chasm by walking the overhead beams, Dez lost his footing and fell. As he fell, flight woke in him. He did not reach the floor. Attempting to cross the central chasm by walking the ceiling beams, Dez lost his footing and fell. Before reaching the floor, flight emerged: he drifted across the gap and landed on the far side. The ability was latent, triggered by the moment of freefall.

Dez Confronts Aeneas at the Printing Press

Developing Aeneas mocks Dez and threatens his mother, but Dez finds the inert trapped compartment and takes the memorial cutout; Dez and Cleke ensnare, gag and drag Aeneas off.

Dez Confronts Aeneas at the Printing Press

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 3 Caducan 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Abandoned Printing Press

Participants: Dez, Cleke, Aeneas Solanaceae

Session: Session 19

What Happened

  • 3 Caducan 301 Bloom: Aeneas Solanaceae wandered for a while, then entered the Abandoned Printing Press in the old scholarly printing district of southeastern Cinquefoil, empty since Loquat was founded. Dez and Cleke got in through a rotted back door and watched him pocket something from a desk. Dez confronted him while Cleke stayed hidden with a bow and Ensnaring Strike ready. Aeneas claimed to be doing survey work for House Potentilla and produced a handful of papers. He mocked Dez as a parentless slum rat from Loquat, called himself a graduate of The Academy and said the Magister was expecting him. When Dez said he would kill him, Aeneas answered that a killing in the city had no justification and would get the whole party thrown out. He threatened Dez's mother in the tenements of what used to be Loquat, along with her friend, the innkeeper she meets with and her landlord, until she was alone and last. He added that Calanthe Orchidaceae was free to keep a lover on the side and that he had no interest in her. His turned-out pockets held only the papers, but Dez passed a DC 20 Insight check, caught him glancing at a bare patch of floor and found a hollow square there. Aeneas broke into a nervous sweat. Dez spent 4 story points (12 down to 8) to auto-crit Investigation: the compartment was a push-click drawer with an explosive trap wired to go off if opened wrong, but the trap was badly installed and inert. Aeneas's earlier panic had been an act, and now he showed real confusion, fear and anger. Dez took the cutout inside, The Face Cut from the Memorial Draft. Cleke wrapped Aeneas's legs in thorns with Ensnaring Strike, and the pair disarmed and gagged him and dragged him back to the others. He now limps.

Consequences

Edmund Names Sable

Canon Edmund Vane identifies the party's new contact as the defected Hive operative Sable, confirms Theron Blackthorn's death by the Roots, and warns that Sable holds the Aether Core and plans to kill the Queen.

Edmund Names Sable

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 24 Pollant 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Blackthorn

Participants: Edmund Vane, Val, Calanthe Orchidaceae, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Vesperae Locke, Spadae

Session: Session 13

What Happened

At the safe house, Edmund Vane identified the infantry sergeant Marett Voss as Sable, a former The Hive operative who defected a month or two prior. Sable had learned that the Hive's goal was saving Queen Acantha Rosaceae rather than killing her, rejected it, and broke away to pursue the Queen's death by turning a god against her. She already holds the stolen The Aether Core; the The Aether Golem body remains at the Blackthorn training grounds. Edmund also told the party that Theron Blackthorn died at the hands of The Roots long ago, which makes Sable's promise of his resurrection a lie she knowingly maintains to steer Cassimir Blackthorn. At the safe house on 25 Pollant 301, with no cure found for the Warm Weed holding the prisoners, Edmund Vane named Sable to the party: the infantry sergeant Maret Voss is the defected The Hive operative who broke with Elara Vane over the question of killing the Queen. Edmund then walked out of the safe house alone. At the Hive safe house, Edmund Vane questioned the party and learned they had freed a god, burned Crown Works Installation Seven, and given The Aether Core to a contact they called new. Edmund named that contact as Sable, a former inner-circle operative of The Hive who had defected a month or two before the session. She broke with the Hive because she rejected its goal of finding a non-lethal means to strip divine power from Queen Acantha Rosaceae. Sable means to kill the Queen by turning a god against her, and she now holds the Core: half of what it takes to kill a god. The other half is The Aether Golem, still at Blackthorn's training grounds. Edmund also confirmed that Theron Blackthorn is dead, taken off the field by The Roots and confirmed dead within the week. Sable's promise to resurrect Theron for Cassimir Blackthorn is a lie she uses to control the boy and leverage his access. The party knows Sable's true identity and that she holds The Aether Core. The Aether Golem body becomes an immediate priority: whoever controls both pieces controls what happens to the god in Blackthorn. The party begins planning to beat Sable to the Golem.

Elara Reveals Her History to the Party

Developing In the Hive Forest Base planning room, Elara discloses she died of the Grey Wilt centuries ago, was resurrected and left immortal by Acantha using Veritas's ledger, and that the Hive's true goal is to free Acantha from divine madness rather than kill her; she assigns the party to stop Cinquefoil's god-extraction before Sable arrives.

Elara Reveals Her History to the Party

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 26 Pollant 301 Bloom

Era:

Location: Hive Forest Base

Participants: Elara Vane, Edmund Vane, Val, Calanthe Orchidaceae, Vesperae Locke, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu

Session: Session 15

What Happened

Consequences

Connections

  • Elara Vane
  • Edmund Vane

Log

Description

In the planning room of the Hive Forest Base, Elara Vane told the party the truth. Centuries ago she died of the Grey Wilt. Queen Acantha Rosaceae, who had watched the gods ignore her people throughout the plague, went beneath Caelrosa, defeated and consumed Veritas the God of Truth, and then used Veritas's ledger to write Elara back to life, leaving her immortal in the process. That ledger is the same one the party stole in the Grand Archives Heist.

Acantha did not stop at Veritas. She waged war on every god she believed had failed her people during the plague. The stolen divine power eventually drove her mad. About five years ago she withdrew from public view and dispatched agents into god-holding city-states to extract their divinities for her. Those agents succeeded in Blackthorn. Cinquefoil is next.

Elara named the Hive's actual mission: to free Acantha from the power tearing apart her sanity, not to end her life. She believes the Queen deserves a second chance, not Sable's death sentence. With the truth on the table, Elara assigned the party to infiltrate Cinquefoil, stop the god-extraction there, and reach the city before Sable does. In the planning room of the Hive Forest Base on 27 Pollant 301, Elara Vane told the truth about herself and Queen Acantha Rosaceae.

Elara had died of The Grey Wilt centuries ago. Acantha, ignored by the gods during the plague, used Veritas's Ledger to write Elara back from death; the resurrection left her immortal (Resurrection of Elara Aurum). Acantha then descended beneath Caelrosa, defeated and consumed Veritas the God of Truth (Consumption of Veritas), and waged war on every god that had failed her people. The stolen divine power drove her mad.

The Hive's true goal is not to kill Acantha but to free her from that power. Elara believes the Queen deserves a second chance rather than the death sentence Sable would carry out. Acantha went into seclusion about five years ago and sent agents into god-holding city-states to extract their gods for her; the operation succeeded in Blackthorn, and Cinquefoil is next.

Elara assigned the party to infiltrate Cinquefoil, stop the god-extraction, and arrive before Sable. Edmund Vane then brought in Lord Corvus Blackthorn, turned against Sable by Cassimir Blackthorn's defection, alongside his white-clad barbarian aid Kefra Chico, who joined the party as a new ally.

Grand Archives Heist

Grand Archives Heist

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 14 Pollant 301 Bloom

Participants: Vesperae Locke, Dez, Val, Kirill Amaterasu, Elara Vane

Session: Session 5

What Happened

At midnight the disguised party entered the Grand Archives and replaced the Ledger of Divine Artifacts with Elara Vane's forgery. The fake triggered an alarm for lacking a divine signature. The party pushed into the Inner Archives, found the Roots' covert base, and recovered the Crown's three surveillance operation documents. They escaped undetected, delivered the real Ledger to Elara Vane, and left Loquat with forged papers before Thorn forces arrived. (Session 5, Session 6)

Homecoming at House Potentilla

Developing Lord Vanda weeps at Calanthe's return and grants the party shelter; Lady Cattelya acts as if she never left; Vanda reveals Cattelya's secret Grey Wilt memorial commission; Calfur sleeps soundly for the first time.

Homecoming at House Potentilla

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 1 Caducan 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: House Potentilla

Participants: Calanthe Orchidaceae, Lord Vanda Potentilla, Lady Cattelya Potentilla, Kirill Amaterasu, Val, Vesperae Locke, Cleke, Calfur

Session: Session 16

What Happened

The party reached House Potentilla by Calanthe's childhood route over the east garden wall. Cleke's wings failed mid-climb; he fell, the noise drew a guard who recognized Calanthe Orchidaceae, and the guard walked the group to the front door instead. Calanthe noted the manor guard count had doubled since she left.

Lord Vanda Potentilla met them at the door and embraced Calanthe in tears. Lady Cattelya Potentilla greeted her as though she had never been gone, asking when she would finish her Florescence studies. Lord Vanda recognized Vesperae Locke as Ms. Locke and extended his protection to the whole party despite knowing of their warrants. He then disclosed Cattelya's secret: a memorial to The Grey Wilt, commissioned by the Queen, part of The Queen's Commission.

In the guest room, the party confirmed their task was to find and stop the Crown's plot to consume the city's sleeping god. They acknowledged they had no leads yet and noted they still needed to find Cassimir Blackthorn. Cleke identified a name he had copied from the Inner Archives as Alithiel, the Keeper of Lost Things. The sleeping god of Cinquefoil was established as Amorphia, god of beauty, splendor, glory, and the arts. The party judged the Potentillas were probably kept in the dark, as Blackthorn had been.

Later that night, Kirill's medicine check found Calfur sleeping soundly and beginning to recover for the first time after weeks of worsening on the road.

Lady Cattelya Forgets Calanthe's Homecoming

Developing On the second morning of Petal Rest, Lady Cattelya brings Calanthe a breakfast tray with no memory of her arrival or their dinner; Althea blames the commission's strain; Lord Vanda is evasive at a guarded terrace breakfast while Calfur eats heartily under the table.

Lady Cattelya Forgets Calanthe's Homecoming

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 2 Caducan 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Potentilla Manor

Participants: Lady Cattelya Potentilla, Calanthe Orchidaceae, Althea, Lord Vanda Potentilla, Calfur, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Cleke

Session: Session 17

What Happened

On the second morning of Petal Rest, Lady Cattelya Potentilla came into Calanthe Orchidaceae's old bedroom at Potentilla Manor with a breakfast tray. She had no memory that Calanthe had arrived the day before or that the two had eaten dinner together, and she hurried off to "speak with your father." Althea, once Cattelya's lady-in-waiting and now assigned to Calanthe, said the lady had been confused like this for about a week and blamed the strain of The Queen's Commission. Calanthe noted that stress had never erased such recent memories before.

Calanthe woke Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, and Cleke in the guest room. Calfur, kept there overnight by Kirill, was already up, lively, and hungry. At breakfast on the manor's second terrace Lord Vanda Potentilla ate alone with the party. Cattelya and Magister Ilex Verbena had eaten hours earlier and gone to their morning session. The guards on the terrace faced inward toward the table, and a house-high scaffold draped in sailcloth stood in the Great Square of Cinquefoil below. Aeneas Solanaceae did not appear. Calfur ate heartily under the table, which he never does when ill. Dez tried to examine him and found nothing.

Vanda gave the party the run of the gardens and library and said Verbena had borrowed most of the library's books on the Wilt for the commission. When Calanthe asked about the commission and about Althea's reassignment, he answered that she was nearing the age to learn the Potentilla trade. Calanthe could see he was holding something back and glancing at the guards, so she let the subject drop.

Lord Vanda's Confession in the Study

Developing On 2 Caducan 301 Bloom Lord Vanda tells Calanthe and the party that every lesson strips Cattelya's memories, that the Crown coerced the house under threat of a garrison, and that Verbena is hunting a person inside House Potentilla.

Lord Vanda's Confession in the Study

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 2 Caducan 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Potentilla Manor

Participants: Lord Vanda Potentilla, Calanthe Orchidaceae, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Cleke

Session: Session 17

What Happened

Althea found Calanthe Orchidaceae and sent her to Lord Vanda Potentilla's study, with instructions to bring only those she trusted with her life. Calanthe brought the whole party: Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, and Cleke.

Behind the study door Vanda stopped pretending. Every lesson costs Lady Cattelya Potentilla memories: vacations, meetings, even household staff. In the first week of the commission they were told her help was the Queen's will, and that the alternative was a garrison on the house. The Potentillas have been stewards of Cinquefoil since before the Empire, he said, and Althea was part of why they chose Calanthe. Calanthe told the party then that she is adopted.

Vanda admitted that Magister Ilex Verbena is looking for a person inside House Potentilla. He would say no more, for fear the Magister could take the answer from Calanthe instead of from her mother. Cattelya is holding on as long as she can to protect them all. The party weighed its options, Dez posing as Cattelya with Alter Self or Cleke slipping into the workroom, and chose to hold its cards for now.

Massacre at Benz

Developing The Queen's Thorns kill all surviving witnesses of the Benz crash and frame the party for the village's destruction

Massacre at Benz

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 7 Pollant 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Benz

Participants: Queen's Thorns

Session: Session 1

What Happened

Following the alien attack on Benz, the Queen's Thorns took statements from every surviving witness, then killed them all. The party noticed and escaped before they could be silenced, becoming the only living witnesses to both the alien attack and the massacre. The Crown's official account labeled the destruction of Benz a terrorist attack carried out by the party. The party was charged with Treason, Sedition, Arson, and further unnamed crimes.

  • The party became wanted criminals throughout Elarosea, charged with Treason, Sedition, Arson, and other crimes.
  • The true cause of Benz's destruction (an alien incursion from a crashed star) is suppressed by the Crown.
  • The party are the only surviving witnesses to both events.

A Thorns commander gave the order. Queen Acantha Rosaceae had been in seclusion for some time and gave no command herself; the commander acted on what they took to be her wishes. The party had just fought the Star-Fallen released by the crash, and the frame-up is recorded in Party Blamed for the Benz Incident.

Party Enters Cinquefoil

Developing Lord Corvus Blackthorn vouches the disguised party through the Cinquefoil gate on Petal Rest; the deferential guard waves them through without a real search.

Party Enters Cinquefoil

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 1 Caducan 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Cinquefoil

Participants: Lord Corvus Blackthorn, Calanthe Orchidaceae, Kirill Amaterasu, Val, Vesperae Locke, Kefra Chico, Cleke

Session: Session 16

What Happened

On the first day of Petal Rest, the party approached the Cinquefoil gate by carriage with Lord Corvus Blackthorn riding alongside. A carefully painted warrant of Calanthe Orchidaceae's face was posted at the gate, labeled treason, sedition, and arson. Val clung to the underside of the carriage; Kirill posed as Kefra Chico's court jester; Vesperae Locke wore a veil; Calanthe feigned sleep against Corvus's shoulder with her face hidden. Corvus vouched for his people. The guard, deferential, waved the carriage through without conducting a real search. Inside the city, Corvus split off for separate business, leaving Kefra Chico with the group.

Party Escapes from Crown Works

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 24 Pollant 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Blackthorn

Participants: Val, Kirill Amaterasu, Calanthe Orchidaceae, Dez, Vesperae Locke, Spadae, Val's Father, Val's Sister

Session: Session 13

What Happened

The party surfaced in a rubble-choked alley in Blackthorn immediately after the Crown Works Installation Seven breach. A dozen drugged prisoners were with them, including Val's Father and Val's Sister (15). City search bells rang while the installation burned. Kirill Amaterasu scouted from the rooftops; Calanthe Orchidaceae read the guard patterns below. They put one wandering guard to sleep before a conditioned prisoner could expose the group, then navigated the cordon street by street until they reached Edmund Vane's safe house. With Crown Works Installation Seven burning at their backs and city bells ringing a search, the party came out into a Blackthorn alley with about twelve prisoners dulled by Warm Weed, among them Val's Father and his 15-year-old sister (Val's Sister). Kirill Amaterasu went ahead on foot, slipped past lantern-carrying guards, and waved the group through. He climbed to the rooftops to map the route, but a guard stepped from a doorway beside the hidden party; a conditioned prisoner drifted toward the lantern before Calanthe Orchidaceae dropped the guard with a Sleep spell. At a shuttered market Calanthe read the patrol rotation and threaded the whole group through unseen to the safe house contact point.

Party Investigates the Potentilla Manor

Developing The party finds silent regimented guards in city green each carrying an identical abjuration aura behind the lapel, the Wilt histories and nursery songbook gone from the library, and learns from the maid Bryony about Cattelya's lapses, the early name bread, and a listening grill off the linen passage.

Party Investigates the Potentilla Manor

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 2 Caducan 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Potentilla Manor

Participants: Calanthe Orchidaceae, Dez, Cleke, Kirill Amaterasu, Bryony, Lord Vanda Potentilla, Magister Ilex Verbena

Session: Session 17

What Happened

Calanthe Orchidaceae saw that Potentilla Manor held far more guards than usual. Beside the familiar house guards stood strangers in Cinquefoil city green with a rigid, regimented bearing unlike the relaxed city watch of Cinquefoil, closer to Blackthorn drill. Dez and Cleke walked past them to test them. The house guards greeted the party warmly. The city-uniformed guards only nodded and would not speak. Cleke cast Detect Magic and found that every one of the extra guards carried an identical faint abjuration aura from something behind the lapel. The house staff carried none.

In the library Calanthe confirmed the Wilt histories were gone, along with an old nursery songbook holding the Wilt's lament ("when the wilt came up the water stairs and the high town barred his door, who sat with the low town through the night"). She took three easy-reading books for herself and explained The Grey Wilt to Cleke: a plague and catastrophe about 300 years ago that Queen Acantha Rosaceae ended before founding the Acanthine era. Outside the workroom she heard Magister Ilex Verbena lecturing on the Wilt. She carried in a tea tray from the kitchen and saw covered drafts on a table and a makeshift classroom with a desk and chalkboard. Verbena said the work went well but the deadline was close.

At the midday meal Verbena arrived with a grey book under her arm and asked Lord Vanda Potentilla for another session with Lady Cattelya at the third bell after noon. She said the unveiling date would be announced the next night, that "her ladyship has not yet heard the whole of the Wilt," and that she would find the party after the session. Vanda reminded everyone to wear masks for that night's masquerade and said Althea was always nearby if Calanthe needed her.

In the kitchen the young maid Bryony told the party that Lady Cattelya Potentilla had called her by her mother's name, though her mother died nine years ago. The lapses began about three weeks earlier, and each lesson leaves the lady exhausted for the rest of the day. Bryony showed them a stale piece of Petal Rest name bread that Cattelya had baked weeks too early, with one name pressed into it twice, as if another dead family member's name had been left off. She then told them of a grill behind the panelling in a room off the linen passage where the lessons can be overheard. Calanthe asked her to keep the matter within the family.

Party Splits Over Sable

Developing After the Golem consumed Valerius, Spadae sided with Sable and left with her; Calanthe, Kirill, and Dez denounced Sable and returned to the Hive safe house.

Party Splits Over Sable

Quick Reference

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Blackthorn Training Grounds

The internal split seeded in Session 13, when Calanthe Orchidaceae refused Sable's pitch outright and others equivocated, had to resolve. Spadae pushed the group to agree to help. The party aligned with Sable, and her guards began hauling The Aether Golem toward its launch point ahead of the demonstration scheduled for 26 Pollant 301. After The Aether Golem consumed Valerius, Sable invited the party to her base. Calanthe Orchidaceae, Kirill Amaterasu, and Dez refused and denounced her. Spadae sided with Sable and departed with her, Cassimir Blackthorn, and the god-filled Golem. Before parting, Sable gave the three refusers a Silver Emblem that summons her when pressed into the floor.

Party Visits the Dreamcatcher

Quick Reference

Era: The Acanthine

Location: The Dreamcatcher

He confirmed that the God of Death was consumed by Queen Acantha Rosaceae, making Sable's promise to resurrect Theron Blackthorn impossible. Kirill Amaterasu asked about saving Calfur and received The Index in return: home of Veritas and the site where the Queen first sealed a god into herself. When pressed on whether to kill the Queen, Nal'Szomn said only that "the line between naivety and hopelessness is almost invisible." The party bought healing potions, a wand of fireballs, and a scroll of Zone of Truth before waking. They entered the subsequent confrontation at Blackthorn Training Grounds with both the truth and the means to compel it. Unable to agree on Sable's offer, Calanthe cast Sleep and pulled the party into The Dreamcatcher. Inside, the dream-merchant Nal'Szomn offered truthful answers for one story point each. Calanthe asked whether Sable could resurrect Theron Blackthorn. The answer was plain: only the god of death can touch the dead, and Queen Acantha Rosaceae has consumed him. Sable's promise to Cassimir Blackthorn is a lie she knows is a lie. The party left the Dreamcatcher and agreed to help. Kirill Amaterasu spent points asking how to cure Calfur. Nal'Szomn confirmed no method exists to release a divinity outright. Any ritual to consume or transfer a divinity must be performed at The Index, the home of Veritas and the site where Queen Acantha Rosaceae first sealed a god into herself. The nascent god inside Calfur feeds on the calf's life force and will kill him when it is born.

Calanthe Orchidaceae asked for the merchant's read on killing the Queen. He declined, saying only that the line between naivety and hopelessness is almost invisible. Spadae took that as a yes.

Before waking, the party bought five Greater Healing potions, one Superior Healing potion, a wand of fireballs for Spadae, and a scroll of Zone of Truth.

Sable Pitches the Party at the Training Grounds

Established Sable intercepts the party after Crown Works, reveals she knows their Hive connection, and pitches using the Aether Golem to kill the Queen; Calanthe refuses, Kirill equivocates, and the session ends with the choice unmade.

Sable Pitches the Party at the Training Grounds

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 24 Pollant 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Blackthorn

Participants: Sable, Calanthe Orchidaceae, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Vesperae Locke, Val, Spadae, Cassimir Blackthorn

Session: Session 13

What Happened

Sable found the party at the training grounds, still dressed for The Aether Golem Demonstration whose Crown handlers had not yet been told was dead. She addressed each party member in turn, naming their individual grievance against Queen Acantha Rosaceae, and gave them until evening to choose a side. As Cassimir Blackthorn arrived, she pressed Kirill Amaterasu in particular to see reason, promised she would see him there that night, and walked through the doors while the guards stepped aside. The guards closed ranks behind her. Kirill Amaterasu and Dez were left outside the sealed doors with the choice still unmade. Sable, operating as Marett Voss, intercepted the party before they could split up. She revealed she knew they worked with the Hive, then made her case: the stolen machine was built to consume a god, and the Queen is a god. Dez challenged her about the earlier crossbow ambush; she said her agents could not tell friend from threat in the dark while tailing one of her operatives, and that anyone she actually wanted dead would already be dead. She dismissed Elara Vane's Hive as all patience and no action, called the plan to save the Queen no plan at all, and argued the machine should be turned back on the Crown.

Calanthe Orchidaceae refused outright, saying the party's purpose is to free gods from torment and protect people. Sable shifted approach and worked through the party one by one, naming the Crown's specific harm to each: Val's family, Vesperae's father, Spade's uncle, Kirill's Verdant Mendicants, the destruction of Loquat, and the incident at Benz. She dismissed the Florescence Calanthe prizes, gave the party until evening to bring an answer, then stepped back as Cassimir Blackthorn arrived, assuring them the boy acts of his own free will.

Kirill Amaterasu told her he was not sure he would do what she asked. Sable urged him to see reason and said she would see him at the training ground that night. She walked through the doors as the guards parted for her; they resumed their posts and sealed the entrance behind her, leaving Kirill and Dez outside with the choice still open. The party has until evening to decide whether to ally with Sable and turn the Aether Golem against the Queen or to hold to Elara Vane's non-lethal plan. Cassimir Blackthorn is already inside the training grounds with Sable. The choice is unresolved at session's end.

Sable Questioned Under Zone of Truth

In-world Date: 24 Pollant 301 Bloom

Under compulsion, Sable admitted she never worked with Theron Blackthorn, only admired him and tracked down how he died: Queen Acantha Rosaceae killed him, and Lord Corvus Blackthorn's inaction let it happen. She held to her promise to return Theron to Cassimir but refused to name how. The confrontation confirmed Theron's True Death as fact and set off the social fracture recorded in Party Splits Over Sable. The party found Sable and Cassimir Blackthorn at the Blackthorn Training Grounds beside The Aether Golem. Calanthe Orchidaceae cast Zone of Truth from the scroll bought at The Dreamcatcher, catching everyone in the circle and tripping alarms.

Under the effect, Sable admitted she had never served under Theron Blackthorn, directly contradicting the story she had told Val. She said she had admired him from a distance. She traced his death to Queen Acantha Rosaceae and named Lord Corvus Blackthorn's inaction as part of the cause. The party pressed her because Edmund Vane had already told them that story was false. She still swore she both intends and is able to return Theron to Cassimir, but would not say how.

Sable answered calmly throughout and apologized for the deception. Cassimir came out of the interrogation trusting her over the party and resented what he saw as an ambush. Pushed by Spadae, the party agreed to help. Her guards began hauling The Aether Golem toward its launch point. See Party Splits Over Sable.

The Face Cut from the Memorial Draft

The Face Cut from the Memorial Draft

Quick Reference

Lady Cattelya Potentilla's draft memorial to The Grey Wilt covered all six tables: a square of hundreds of detailed portraits around one faceless woman standing above the crowd. Cattelya had rubbed that face out so many times the paper had worn through.

The face cut out of Lady Cattelya Potentilla's memorial draft. Aeneas Solanaceae hid it in a push-click drawer under a bare patch of floor in the Abandoned Printing Press, wired to an explosive trap meant to go off if the drawer was opened wrong. The trap was badly installed and inert.

The Grey Wilt

Developing Plague from the start of the Acanthine era; the subject of Lady Cattelya's memorial commission.

The Grey Wilt

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 1 Pollant Bloom

Era: The Unrecorded

Location:

Participants: Queen Acantha Rosaceae, Elara Vane

Session:

What Happened

A disease spread through the lower quarters of the city, killing many. Among the apparent dead was Elara Aurum, a cartographer's apprentice and the person Acantha loved in secret. Acantha prayed to every god whose domain might have helped. None moved. She went last to Veritas and asked only for the truth of whether Elara was alive or dead. He kept it, as he kept all things, written and shut away. Veritas's silence resolved Acantha into action. If the gods would hoard their power and spend none of it, she would take a god's power and spend all of it. Within a year she had consumed Veritas, beginning the calendar recorded as Year 1 of the Bloom. The plague moved fast through the lower quarters, where crowding and shared water gave it easy ground. Higher up, where people had room and clean water, it was slow and often survivable. The cartographers' district where Elara Aurum apprenticed sat near the bottom. She fell ill, was carried out with a day's dead, and was written into the death tally before anyone confirmed she had stopped breathing. She survived in a fever-ward she was never recorded as entering, weeks of it, while Acantha above buried an empty name and turned cold and certain. The Wilt is two tragedies wearing one face. It gave Acantha her case against the gods: no divine power bent to stop it, and it killed hardest in the quarters where people had the least. It is also a clerical error, a name in the wrong column entered in haste, that convinced two people each other was dead (see Elara Aurum Lives). Acantha consumed Veritas to keep this from happening again. She does not know it never quite happened to Elara at all. The Grey Wilt was a catastrophe, almost certainly a plague, that struck centuries before 301 Bloom. Elara Vane died of it before Queen Acantha Rosaceae resurrected her. The Crown has an ongoing interest in the event: Lady Cattelya Potentilla is secretly building a memorial commissioned by the Queen, with Magister Ilex Verbena traveling from the capital to assist. The Potentillas appear to have been kept in the dark about the commission's deeper purpose. The Grey Wilt was a plague at the start of The Acanthine era. Lady Cattelya Potentilla has been working on a memorial to it, and the Wilt histories have gone missing from the Potentilla Manor library.

  • 3 Caducan 301 Bloom: At breakfast on the second terrace, Lady Cattelya described The Queen's Commission to the party as a mural of the Grey Wilt, a sign of how far her memory has slipped (Lady Cattelya Forgets Bryony, Session 18).

Consequences

  • pre-1 Bloom: Elara Aurum died during the plague. Queen Acantha Rosaceae subsequently wrote her resurrection into Veritas's Ledger, setting the contradiction in motion that now makes Elara unkillable. See Resurrection of Elara Aurum.

Connections

  • Queen Acantha Rosaceae
  • Elara Vane
  • Veritas
  • Session 15
  • The Queen's Commission

Log

  • 2 Caducan 301 Bloom: Calanthe Orchidaceae tells Cleke the Wilt was a plague and catastrophe about 300 years ago, ended by Queen Acantha Rosaceae before she founded The Acanthine era. The Wilt histories are gone from the Potentilla Manor library, and so is the nursery songbook holding the lament ("when the wilt came up the water stairs and the high town barred his door, who sat with the low town through the night"). Through the East Wing grill the party hears Magister Ilex Verbena lecture Lady Cattelya Potentilla on how the Wilt came up the water stairs. That night Cattelya's memorial draft lies uncovered on six tables: hundreds of portraits around one faceless woman above the crowd, the newest face cut out with a knife and taken (The Face Cut from the Memorial Draft, Session 17).

Description

The Grey Wilt was a plague that swept through the mortal world centuries ago, killing vast numbers while the gods remained silent. Elara Vane, then known as Elara Aurum, died of it and was later written back to life by Queen Acantha Rosaceae using Veritas's ledger (Resurrection of Elara Aurum). Acantha, enraged that the gods ignored her people's suffering, descended beneath Caelrosa, defeated and consumed Veritas (Consumption of Veritas), then waged war on every god that had failed her world. The divine power she seized eventually drove her mad. Both The Hive's mission and Sable's opposing plan trace directly to those years of indifferent gods and mass death. A wasting disease with a presence in historical records. It killed Elara Aurum (later known as Elara Vane) centuries ago. Acantha resurrected Elara using Veritas's ledger afterward, leaving her immortal. The disease's broader history, cause, and spread are unrecorded. An ancient plague that killed Elara Aurum, among others, and went unanswered by the gods. Queen Acantha Rosaceae watched her people die while divinity offered nothing, and that rage drove her to descend beneath Caelrosa and consume Veritas the God of Truth. The Wilt is the root cause of Acantha's war on the gods and, by extension, the founding purpose of The Hive.

The Party Spares Aeneas

Developing After reading Sable's letter, Calanthe refuses to kill Aeneas; the party chooses cunning over Sable or the Queen's Thorns and sends word to Elara, Sirocco and Cassimir.

The Party Spares Aeneas

Quick Reference

In-world Date: 3 Caducan 301 Bloom

Era: The Acanthine

Location: Cinquefoil

Participants: Calanthe Orchidaceae, Dez, Kirill Amaterasu, Cleke, Val, Vesperae Locke, Kefra Chico, Aeneas Solanaceae

Session: Session 19

What Happened

The group picked up Vesperae Locke in the market district of Cinquefoil and met Dez and Cleke, who had dragged Aeneas Solanaceae there disarmed and gagged. Cleke's Ensnaring Strike had wrapped his legs in thorns, and he now limps.

Kirill Amaterasu handed Calanthe Orchidaceae the open Letter from S to Aeneas, in which Sable offered riches and power in exchange for spying on House Potentilla. Dez and Kirill pushed to kill Aeneas and dump him in the river. Calanthe refused and tended his wounds.

The party weighed siding with Sable or the Queen's Thorns. They recalled that their last deal with Sable cost them The Aether Golem and Spadae, and decided to play it cunning rather than aggressive. Dez checked Sable's Meeting House in Cinquefoil and found Sable gone. The party messaged Elara Vane by the method she had given them, Dez paid 4 story points (8 down to 4) to send a letter to Sirocco, and Calanthe wrote Calanthe's Letter to Cassimir to Cassimir Blackthorn (Persuasion 22).

Theft of the Aether Core

Established Maret Voss and Cassimir Blackthorn flee Crown Works Installation Seven with the Aether Core while the party is occupied in the vault below.

Theft of the Aether Core

Quick Reference

Location: Crown Works Installation Seven

Participants: Maret Voss, Cassimir Blackthorn

Session: Session 12

What Happened

While the party fought Roots Arcanist Quill, Sable's men pried the Aether Core free from its energy pylons in the sub-floor laboratory of Crown Works Installation Seven. During the work, she told Cassimir Blackthorn that the Blackthorn Writings are his brother's doing, that Lord Corvus Blackthorn signed the death warrant, and that she can bring Theron Blackthorn back by harnessing the god in Blackthorn (corrected 2026-07-12: she never claimed Theron is alive). Once Quill was dead, she and Cassimir took the core and fled ahead of the party, leaving the building on fire behind them. In the underground lab beneath Crown Works Installation Seven, Maret Voss's men pried the dodecahedron Aether Core free from the energy pylons holding it in place. While the extraction was underway, Maret told Cassimir she can bring Theron back by harnessing the god in Blackthorn (corrected 2026-07-12: she never claimed Theron is alive) and told Val that the Hive intended the core to leave with her so the Crown could not use it. Roots Arcanist Quill emerged as the operation was nearly complete and combat began.

  • 24 Pollant 301: While the party engaged Roots Arcanist Quill and vault guards, Maret Voss's three crew members unsealed the central housing and passed the core to her. She and Cassimir Blackthorn fled Crown Works Installation Seven, calling out that a ship would be changing over soon. Fire the party had set above tripped the facility alarm as they left, threatening a guard shift rotation.

When the party climbed the ladder out of the vault after killing Roots Arcanist Quill, they found Maret Voss and Cassimir Blackthorn already gone, having taken the Aether Core with them. Their route and destination are unknown.
